# Signal Bridge — project map

> **For AI agents:** Read this file first when working on the NAS/container code.  
> **Keep fresh:** Update this file whenever you change architecture, modules, config, Docker, auth, or UDP behavior. Bump **Last updated** and add a line under **Recent changes**.

**Last updated:** 2026-09-02 (manual push actor stamp)

---

## What this is

A **Node.js service (Signal Bridge)** that bridges household services to smart displays: Alexa voice (via `alexa-remote2`), Tesla/Vivint and more, **UDP** overlays to LAN clients, the **Signal** phone UI (`src/web/`), and a **display registry** from client announces.

There is **no supported Amazon API** for passive broadcast listening. Detection uses Alexa **push events** + **voice history polling** and heuristics in `parser.js`.

User-facing overview: repo root `README.md`. Docker: `DOCKER.md`.

---

## System context

```
Echo / Alexa app  →  Amazon cloud  →  alexa-remote2 (this bridge)
                                              │
        ┌─────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┐
        ▼                                     ▼                                     ▼
 data/voice-events.jsonl              HTTPS :47810                           UDP :47832
 data/alexa-session.json              control web (src/web/)                 overlays / commands
 data/displays-registry.json                  │                                     │
                                              │                                     ▼
                                              │                           Windows client(s)
                                              │                           (overlays, WebView2,
                                              │                            remote input)
                                              │                                     │
                                              └──── UDP :47833 ◄── display.announce ─┘
```

**Typical deployment:** QNAP NAS, Docker, `network_mode: host`, `./data` volume for session + config.

---

## Repository layout (bridge)

| Path | Role |
|------|------|
| `src/index.js` | Entry: loads config, starts listener, auth-error backoff |
| `src/listener.js` | Core orchestrator: Alexa init, events, history polls, health, keep-alive. `sendUdpPayload` also offers the payload to `vestaboardHub.pushEvent` (returns that fan-out on the result); a target that is only a board skips UDP and `displayBusy`. Scheduler airings stamp `source: 'scheduler'` / `triggeredBy` so the board queue can enforce `minRotationGapSeconds` |
| `src/parser.js` | Detects announce/broadcast utterances; two-step prompt pairing (same- or cross-device when Alexa confirms); never treats “Announcing on all devices” TTS as the message; `historyPollStartMs` keeps a 2-min overlap after the last capture so a newer one-shot cannot hide an earlier follow-up; uses `broadcast-parse` to prefer a single ASR fragment and strip duplicated `", broadcast …"` echoes |
| `src/broadcast-parse.js` | Parse/clean broadcast utterances (`resolveBroadcastUtterance`, `cleanBroadcastMessage`) — Amazon often stores wake+repeat ASR that used to display as `"msg, broadcast msg"` or `", broadcast"` |
| `src/session.js` | Load/save `alexa-session.json`; `buildAlexaInitOptions` for listener vs auth |
| `src/session-keepalive.js` | Auth ping, token refresh (via ping cycle), liveness probe, proactive refresh |
| `src/auth-refresh-patch.js` | Replaces broken `alexa-cookie2` refresh with vendored skip-register version |
| `src/vendor/alexa-cookie-refresh.js` | Patched cookie refresh: no `/auth/register` during refresh (fixes 24h auth loss) |
| `src/session-auth-journal.js` | Append-only JSONL auth event log with failure classification |
| `src/session-meta.js` | Token age / session metadata helpers |
| `src/error-format.js` | Unwrap AggregateError and nested causes for clearer logs |
| `src/auth.js` | One-off Amazon login via local proxy (`npm run auth`) |
| `src/auth-proxy-patch.js` | Replaces stock `alexa-cookie2` proxy with vendored version |
| `src/vendor/alexa-cookie-proxy.js` | Patched login proxy (font fixes, static assets, UI CSS injection) |
| `src/port-utils.js` | Pre-check port 3456 before auth proxy bind |
| `src/auth-status.js` | Writes `data/auth-status.json` when session expires |
| `src/broadcast-udp.js` | UDP send (broadcast / unicast) on `:47832`; listen for `display.announce` on `:47833` (`udpBroadcast.discoveryPort`); seals/opens via `lan-crypto` when `LAN_UDP_SECRET` is set. Untargeted `sendUdpPayload` (YouTube/Steam/PSN lounge, Alexa broadcasts) now unicasts to every announced full-display host via `displayRegistry.withRegisteredUdpHosts` — broadcast-only often never reached the poster PC while Vestaboard still flipped over HTTP |
| `src/lan-crypto.js` | Shared-secret **AES-256-GCM** for bridge↔display UDP (`LAN_UDP_SECRET` / `udpBroadcast.sharedSecret`); protocol v3 envelope `{v,alg,n,c}`; SHA-256 key derive; stamps `sentAt` at seal; ±120s freshness on `sentAt` (not Alexa activity `timestamp`) |
| `src/steam-*.js` | Steam Now Playing: config/session/OpenID auth (callback requires one-time `state` from admin start), Web API + store appdetails, presence allowlist, poller with interrupt-suppress, UDP builders |
| `src/psn-*.js` | PSN Now Playing (unofficial `psn-api`): NPSSO → tokens in `data/psn-session.json`, `getBasicPresence` poller, played-games/trophy enrich, fail-soft Chihiro Store Plan B (`psn-store.js` — description + real screenshots + stars), Admin NPSSO paste + manual preview, UDP `psn.now-playing` |
| `src/plex-api.js` | **Feature Presentation** — `GET {serverUrl}/status/sessions` with `X-Plex-Token` (8s timeout); parses `MediaContainer.Metadata[]` into session rows. No webhooks. Token never logged. `fetchPlexJson(url, …)` is the shared authenticated GET, used for the local server and the cloud providers |
| `src/plex-credentials.js` | Plex token via `secret-box.js`: `PLEX_TOKEN` env wins, else encrypted `data/plex-credentials.json`. Same 409-if-env pattern as Guardian/YouTube |
| `src/plex-settings.js` | Live-reload `data/plex-settings.json` (seeded from `config.plex`); `enabled`, `serverUrl`, `monitoredPlayers[]`, `mediaTypes` (default `movie`), poll/stop-grace, `pushOnStop`, `quietHoursExempt`, `showCriticScore`. Never writes `config.json` |
| `src/plex-now-playing.js` | Theater watcher: poll 15s, filter type + player IP, persist `data/plex-now-playing.json`, fan-out **Vestaboard only** (`targetId: 'vestaboard'`). Pause ≠ stop; poll failures ≠ stop (`missingSince` is last successful presence). A `playing` session whose `viewOffset` does not move for `stopGraceMs` is treated as a stop (Apple TV power-off leaves a Plex zombie). Restart with the same `sessionKey` re-posts and keeps `startedAt`. Movie switch emits stop then play without a LAST PLAYED flash. Live events carry `quietHoursExempt` from settings; scheduler pushes force it off |
| `src/ring-doorbell.js` | **Ring Doorbell** — `ring-client-api` refresh-token session; subscribe to ding (+ optional motion); fan-out Vestaboard-only `ring.doorbell` alerts with a yellow-bell / red-rail card. Title/message from settings. Auth: `RING_REFRESH_TOKEN` or encrypted `data/ring-credentials.json` |
| `src/ring-settings.js` | Live-reload `data/ring-settings.json` (enabled, title, message, pushOnDing/Motion, quietHoursExempt, cameraIds) |
| `src/ring-credentials.js` | Ring refresh token via `secret-box.js`; env wins (409 overwrite) |
| `src/activity-fields.js` | Harvest summary/response/allText from all `voiceHistoryRecordItems` types (app routines often skip ASR) |
| `src/routine-index.js` | Cache `getAutomationRoutines()`; map name/trigger/action phrases → voice kinds; resolve bare “Sent to Display” |
| `src/plex-top10.js` | **Plex Top 10 Movies** (Vestaboard only). Two sources: `library` sorts the house movie section by `viewCount:desc` and drops unwatched rows; `global` walks Plex Discover's chart hubs (`DISCOVER_HUBS`, tried in order — those keys are undocumented, hence the fallbacks). Genres are optional and empty means all: library filters server-side by tag id, global filters on whatever `Genre` tags the hub returned and reports `genresApplied: false` when it sent none. Borrows the Feature Presentation server URL and token; in-memory cache for `cacheMinutes` (default 180) |
| `src/display-voice-commands.js` | Matchers for Alexa routines that push display overlays without needing Alexa’s spoken answer: trivia, Steam/PSN library tours, Steam/PSN/YouTube now-or-last-played (`requestedMode: 'auto'`), Feature Presentation (`feature presentation` / `plex now playing` / `what's playing in the theater` — Vestaboard only) |
| `src/upside-news-*.js` | **The Upside News** — Guardian + positive RSS archive, period selection, filters/ranking, settings, credentials (`GUARDIAN_API_KEY` / encrypted `data/guardian-credentials.json`), UDP `upside-news.round`; topic artwork under `/upside-news-artwork/` |
| `src/wiki-common-knowledge-*.js` | **Wiki Common Knowledge** — Wikimedia featured/pageviews + summary/history, day-list cache under `data/wiki-common-knowledge-cache/`, settings in `data/wiki-common-knowledge-settings.json` (required contact email for User-Agent), denylist + description→topic map, UDP `wiki-common-knowledge.round`; artwork under `/wiki-common-knowledge-artwork/` |
| `src/overhead-*.js` | **Overhead (flight radar)** — public ADS-B fetch (adsb.lol → adsb.fi → airplanes.live; the last now 403s unregistered clients), adsbdb route/type enrichment, settings in `data/overhead-settings.json`, live session poller (`overhead.round` / `overhead.update` / `overhead.close`); home lat/lon from Global Settings (`data/locale-settings.json` → `voiceEvents.defaultLocation`); static GeoJSON at `/overhead-geo/` |
| `src/web/overhead-geo/` | Placeholder `home-area.json` + sample `airports.json` for Overhead scope map |
| `src/web/wiki-common-knowledge-artwork/` | Topic JPEGs (20 categories) for Wiki Common Knowledge (seeded from Upside/trivia packs when missing) |
| `src/web/upside-news-artwork/` | Shipped topic JPEGs (13 × portrait/landscape) for The Upside News |
| `src/vestaboard/encoder.js` | **Vestaboard** character set: `fold` (uppercase, strip accents, transliterate, substitute punctuation), `encodeText`/`decodeCodes`, `wrap` (greedy with orphan-aware breaks: short ≤2-char glue words move onto the next line; hyphenates only over-long words; `{ orphans: false }` fills every line instead — see `roast.me`), `truncate`, row helpers, `validate`/`assertValidLayout` (6×22, legal codes only — the board's six reserved codes are rejected), `drumOrder()` (legal codes in module walk order), and the house number formats (`formatAverage`/`formatWhole`/`formatCount`, absent → `''` not `0`) |
| `src/vestaboard/notation.js` | Parse/print the chip-letter layout notation used by the golden fixtures (`parseLayout`, `formatLayout`) |
| `src/vestaboard/frames.js` | Frame builders: `badgeFrame` (corner chips + title/body/footer), `borderFrame` (solid alert border, optional "more" marker), `cinemaFrame` (red curtain, 20-col **right-biased** center, `full` vs `sides` rails; whitespace-only parts become `BLANK` because `fold()` would eat `' '`), `gauge` (battery bar), `blockTime` (block-digit clock), plus `lr`, `centered`, `pageCounter`, `dwellFor` |
| `src/vestaboard/index.js` | Wires the feature: owns the board list, **one house queue**, and a transport per enabled board. Posted pages fan out to every enabled board (a single 503 does not stall the house; lagging boards catch up to the current page). Adopts the simulator by walking its enablement handshake at **every** boot. Exposes `registryEntries()`, `settingsView()`, `houseSettings()`, `pushEvent()`, `submit()`, and `testFlip()` (test flip still hits one board). Starts the Quiet Hours Reminder watcher |
| `src/vestaboard/priorities.js` | House-wide jump / **immediate (Now)** / hold catalog built from every vestaboard-capable Push command plus live interrupts. House defaults (`recommended`): household interrupts **jump + Now**; Word Scramble / Huupe / Autodarts **hold** with a 30-minute safety timeout. Jump without Now waits out the page already showing. Unlisted events queue. `null` → defaults; `[]` → nothing jumps. Hold minutes clamp 1–180. Omitted `immediate` on a saved rule still means cut in |
| `src/vestaboard/holds.js` | Structural classify + `priorities.applyPolicy`. **Jump** goes to the front; **immediate** also replaces what is showing (alert lane / clear dwell). **Hold** (game lane) owns the house line until the session ends, a higher-listed jumper arrives, or the timeout fires. YouTube / Plex / Steam / PSN are rotation unless the house list says otherwise. Close types release a source even with no card |
| `src/vestaboard/router.js` | The `(event type, display kind)` table. Merges every formatter map, aliases Push command ids onto UDP types, and `routeEvent()` formats **once** and `submit`s **once** to the house queue. Lane comes from `holds.classify` plus house priorities. Close payloads call `submit` with empty frames. No formatter → one debug line per board and no HTTP. Empty `[]` → silent skip (unless it is a close). `full` is UDP-only; `all` / `vestaboard` / a single board id all mean every enabled board |
| `src/vestaboard/replay.js` | `npm run board-replay` — feeds a slice of `voice-events.jsonl` through the real router at speed, aimed at a **temporary** simulator (not the live :7000 board). Exit 0 when every produced frame was accepted; skip empty / no-formatter. `--rate-window 0` for CI |
| `src/vestaboard/transport.js` | HTTP client for the Local API. Identical code for hardware and simulator — only `baseUrl` and key differ. Returns a **classified** outcome (`ok`/`busy`/`auth`/`layout`/`server`/`network`) instead of throwing, because each means something different to the queue |
| `src/vestaboard/queue.js` | One **house** queue — the heart of the feature. Pacing (`rateWindowSeconds` is the Local API / flap cooldown; Settings → **Dwell** (`dwellSeconds`) is how long a rotation page stays — snapshots wait `max(rate window, house dwell)` via `snapshotUntil`; sequenced pages also floor `notBefore` at house dwell so a 15s formatter stamp cannot undercut Settings → Dwell; alerts and live hold cards still wait only the rate window). **Jump vs hold** (`holds.js` + house `priorities`): a jumper goes to the front and does **not** discard waiting pages (restore `lastSnapshot` only when the queue is empty or everything left is parked behind a still-active lock); **Now** (`immediate`) also drops current dwell and flips as soon as the flap window allows — an in-flight snapshot post must not put dwell back on top of that jumper; after its dwell the queue continues. A **hold** lock (`laneLock`, still exposed as `gameLock`) parks lower-rank pages until the session ends, a higher-listed **holding** jumper takes over, a non-holding jumper (doorbell / alarm) **ends** the lock so the queue continues, or the safety timeout fires — Word Scramble still takes it via `acquireGameLock` / `hub.setGameLock`; Huupe and Autodarts acquire it from the classified payload when the house list marks them as holds; now-playing does not hold unless configured. Score/metadata updates of the same source **replace** the pending live card. Close payloads release and drop that source. Relative rank is the house Priorities list (top first). The lock is session-scoped, not derived from `holdSeconds`. Game phases still line up via `replaceSource` / `replaceCard` / `gameSource`. **`holdKind: guest`** parks scheduler snapshots only. **Manual Push / Air now join the back of the line**; only an explicit takeover (Word Scramble invite, first guest-book page) jumps ahead. `cancel(id)` / `reorder(ids)` let the simulator drop or drag pages. **Quiet hours** (only `alarm.fired`/`timer.fired` pass; `options.quietHoursExempt` opts a caller in). Serialized `tick()`, `setTransport()`, and the retry/health ladder: 503 waits out the window, network/5xx backs off 30s → 60s → 5min with `unhealthy` after three, 401 goes `degraded`. After a recreate, `lastPostAt` is restored from `data/vestaboard-runtime.json` so the first tick does not 503 |
| `src/vestaboard/settings.js` | Board list plus `house: { dwellSeconds, priorities }` in `data/vestaboard-settings.json`; keys encrypted through `secret-box.js`. Seeds the simulator board on first boot; it can be switched off but not removed. Per-board `quietHours` includes `remindOnStart` (default on). Legacy per-board dwell/priorities migrate onto `house` on load. Board upsert does not touch house fields |
| `src/vestaboard/clock.js` | Time wording for 22 columns: `clockLabel` (`9:56PM`, no space), `dateLabel` (`SUNDAY AUG 23`), `dayPhrase` (`TOMORROW` / weekday inside the week / date past it), `countdown` (`12:34`, `1:22:05`), `durationLabel` (`23H 57M` — two units, never three, because a third overflows a 16-column footer), `partOfDay`. Instants take optional `{ timeZone }` (hub injects `houseTimeZone(config)`, default `America/Denver`) so a UTC Docker host prints Utah wall time; omit the zone and the process clock is used. `spanSeconds` guards the `Number(null) === 0` trap so a missing remaining-time prints nothing rather than a confident `0:00`. `parseYmd` reads `YYYY-MM-DD` as a local calendar date so a beat date or forecast day does not slip west of UTC |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/common.js` | Shared envelope for every family: `snapshotFrame` / `alertFrame`, `paginate`, `padRows`, and `isPlaceholderTitle` (Steam `App 123`, PSN `PlayStation Game`, Roll Credits `Unknown game`) |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/alexa.js` | **Alexa family (03 §A)** — formatters keyed by UDP payload type, dispatched by `framesFor(payload, ctx)`. Every one returns an array: `[]` means "not worth flipping the board for", and the all-quiet frames (timers, alarms, shopping) only appear when `ctx.explicit`, never in the ambient rotation. Broadcasts are the priority feature: violet border, wrapped at 18, three tiers by line count (≤3 → room name on the last row, exactly 4 → the words win and the room yields, ≥5 → paged with the yellow continuation chip). `fitDevice` abbreviates before it cuts, so "Master Bathroom Echo" reads `MASTER BATH ECHO` rather than `MASTER BATHRO`. Fire frames set `source: 'timer.fired'` / `'alarm.fired'` because the queue reads that first word to decide what may pass quiet hours. **Weekly Weather Report** (`weather.weekly`) is the marketplace two-column board: `WEATHER REPORT` + city, then `WED    59F` + a condition chip (orange/yellow/blue/violet/red). Today is top-left; days fill the left column then the right |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/tesla.js` | **Tesla family (03 §B)** — dashboard (`payload.dashboard`, not a flat object) and battery (`payload.battery`, not `reading`). Stale is `stale && !refreshing`; `freshnessSec` is recomputed on every cache read and is not a staleness signal. An error dashboard has no `vehicle`/`battery` and returns `[]` rather than a blank Tesla. The driving chip has two formats (`0 mph · Park` vs `Heading NW · 65 mph · Drive`) |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/gaming.js` | **Gaming family (03 §C)** — Steam/PSN (`mode` is `playing`/`last-played`; `library-tour` is not board-capable), Autodarts live/finish/dashboard, Huupe live/final/dashboard, Roll Credits. Live Steam/PSN/Huupe/Autodarts cards are **snapshots**; the queue's hold lane (not `priority: alert`) keeps them on the board and coalesces score updates. Autodarts finish reads `players[].isWinner` (the match payload does not copy `winner`). Dashboard records are `{value, player}` objects, not numbers. Roll Credits latest is `max(induction)` and the footer uses the system *label*, not the id. Names truncate at 13 |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/feeds.js` | **Feeds family (03 §D)** — YouTube (counts already on the payload; hidden counts are `null` never `0`; device label only when `ctx.deviceStatus` is `linked` or omitted), The Upside, Wiki, Overhead (routes live in `routes[hex]`, not on the aircraft), trivia (gated: two-column / stacked / boolean, or skip), Flight Plan tracker (`flightPlanBoardFrames`: trip name on the badge, then flight number + countdown, origin/dest, 12h clocks, status with gate on the right when it fits; trip board pages up to 4), **Learn Japanese** (`japanese.learn`: hinomaru chips, `WORD:` / `MEANS:` when they fit, `(POS)`, `LEVEL: N#`, left-leaning centre), **Learn Portuguese / Spanish / French / German / Italian** (`*.learn`: same card, flag-coloured chips, CEFR `LEVEL: A1`/`A2`), **Quiet Hours Reminder** (`quiet-hours.reminder`: palm sunset / activated / stars plus house extras), **Chuck Norris Fun Facts** (`chuck.facts`: orange title chips, left-aligned joke), **Roast Me!** (`roast.me`: no title, left-aligned block centred down all six rows, greedy wrap), **Family Quotes** (`family.quotes`: no title, a row per sentence at 21 columns, attribution on the last sentence's tail), **Misheard Lyrics** (`misheard.lyrics`: no title, two-column indent, lyric then `- ARTIST`, one frame), **Dad Jokes** (`dad.jokes`: no title, setup / blank row / punchline, both halves greedy-wrapped at 21 columns, block centred), **US State Facts** (`state.facts`: chip-flanked name, CAPITAL/BIRD/FLOWER two-column wrap at flap 10), **US Weather Map** (`us.weather-map`: 89 colour chips in the shape of the lower 48, no text, blanks stay blank), **Word Riddles** (`word.riddles`: green/blue intro, airy riddle centred on all six rows, letter-spaced answer — no bezel wordmark on the flaps), **Amazing Facts** (`amazing.facts`: yellow `AMAZING FACT` chips, left-aligned quirk), **World Geography Facts** (`geo.facts`: green `WORLD GEOGRAPHY` chips, left-aligned quirk), **Conversation Starters** (`talk.starters`: violet `LET'S TALK` chips, left-aligned prompt), **Stoic Quotes** (`stoic.quotes`: white `STOIC` chips, left-aligned quote, right-aligned author), **On This Day in History** (`history.day`: red `ON THIS DAY` chips, centered date/year, left-aligned event), **Baking Inspiration** (`bake.inspire`: yellow `BAKE THIS` chips, title + ≤5 ingredients), **World Population Tracker** (`world.population`: green chips, comma-formatted estimate, birth/death + net rates), **Calendar Clock** (`calendar.clock`: 7-col month chips, `SMTWTFS` only on 5-week months, per-month colour + contrasting today, weekday / month+day / 12h clock), **Word Clock** (`word.clock`: the time as a centred sentence, rows built in `word-clock.js`). Trivia wire format is `answers` + `correctIndex`, not `correctAnswer` |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/signal.js` | Frames about the bridge itself — the Test flip identity frame (board name plus one pass of every chip colour, a mechanical self-check), **guest snaps** (SSID + password typed out because a board cannot show a QR), **guest book** (`guest.book` message + optional footer page, normal snapshot dwell, no hold) and **guest book invite** (`guest.book.invite` — `SIGN THE` / `GUEST BOOK` plus the chip parade and TinyURL), **Ring Doorbell** (`ring.doorbell` alert from painted rows) |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/cinema.js` | **Feature Presentation** — cinema NOW PLAYING / LAST PLAYED / empty (`FEATURE PRESENTATION` header, 20 chars). Title fit: one line / drop trailing parenthetical / split minimizing the longer line (ties keep more on top) / hyphen-split 21-char words. Layout B may drop spaces around the white rating chip. Snapshots, not alerts — no pin. Never a full-display formatter |
| `src/vestaboard/generate-flip-wav.js` | Converts `dev assets/vestaboard-requirements/vestaboard-sample.wav` to mono PCM `src/web/admin/vb-flip.wav` (~5.6s) for the simulator |
| `src/vestaboard/simulator.js` | Stand-in board speaking the **real Local API** on its own port (default **7000**): `POST /local-api/enablement`, `POST`/`GET /local-api/message`. Same headers and failures as hardware — 401 without the key, 400 on an unshowable layout, 503 inside the rate window or when toggled off; identical re-posts are accepted without re-flipping and without restarting the window. State (key, current face) in `data/vestaboard-simulator.json`; the key and enablement token never reach a log, an SSE frame, or an API response |
| `src/unmatched-activity-log.js` | Cap-append `data/unmatched-activities.jsonl` for unmatched history rows (debug app Runs) |
| `tools/steam-presence-reporter/` | **Optional** fallback only — normally presence is piggybacked on the theater PC’s `display.announce` (`hostname` + `steamAppId`) |
| `src/command-registry.js` | **Single source of truth for pushable pages.** `COMMANDS[]` descriptors (`id`, `title`, `group`, `route`, `icon`, `body`, `pushable`, `schedulable`, `supportsContentCheck`, `variableDuration`, `defaultDurationSeconds`, `params`, `kinds`); `BOARD_COMMAND_IDS` marks what a Vestaboard can show (photos and library tours stay `full` only; Feature Presentation, **Weekly Weather Report**, **Weather Alerts**, **Learn Japanese**, **Quiet Hours Reminder**, **Chuck Norris Fun Facts**, **Word Riddles**, **Word Scramble**, **Amazing Facts**, **World Geography Facts**, **Conversation Starters**, **Stoic Quotes**, **On This Day in History**, **Baking Inspiration**, **Stock Market**, **World Currency Rates**, **World Population Tracker**, **Calendar Clock**, **Word Clock**, and **Guest Book Invite** are `vestaboard` only). `PUSH_CATEGORIES` are the Push tab's sub-tabs (`home`, `games`, `media`, `news`, `language`, `travel`, `share`) and `pushCategoryOf()` resolves one per command — by group, with per-id exceptions (Alexa's Now Playing files under Media, not Home). `createCommandRegistry(deps)` binds live state for `hasContent(id)` / `estimateDuration(id, params)`. Served at `GET /api/commands` (including `pushCategory`); the admin Push grid filters tiles by the selected display's kind. `assertValid()` rejects duplicate ids, duration contradictions, unknown kinds, and a pushable command with no category to land in |
| `src/display-registry.js` | Known displays from announces; persist `data/displays-registry.json`; prune after ~12 min without re-announce; **discover sweep** drops silent displays after Refresh (~2.5s); resolve target → unicast host. Announced entries read as `kind: 'full'`; the optional `staticEntries` provider merges in **Vestaboards** (`kind: 'vestaboard'`), which are configured rather than discovered — never persisted here, never pruned. `resolveDelivery` class targets: `all` (UDP + boards), `full` (UDP only, `target.class: 'full'`), `vestaboard` (HTTP only). A board id returns `kind: 'vestaboard'` and empty UDP `sendOptions` (not an error — the listener routes that target over HTTP). `withRegisteredUdpHosts` fills unicast IPs for untargeted lounge/broadcast sends so Vestaboard HTTP is not the only path that lands |
| `src/message-details.js` | Parse sender/destination/message for broadcast payloads |
| `src/udp-payload.js` | Build typed UDP payloads (broadcast, time, weather, indoor temperature, timer, reminder, `qr.display`, `guest.photobooth`, `input.text`, `photo.slideshow`, `route-planner.query`) |
| `src/voice-query-parser.js` | Detect time/weather/indoor temperature/timer/reminder/music/route/guest-photobooth/trivia/library-tour/platform-now-playing voice queries from history |
| `src/voice-event-dedup.js` | Suppress re-displays: activity-id + 2-min window, 30-min instants, content keys for vivint/notifications; **smart-home** keys on device+action+target so Amazon’s delayed `lights on` fragment does not replay |
| `src/public-url.js` | **Public base URL** — live-reload `data/public-url-settings.json` (not `config.json`). Getter re-reads; `applyToConfig` copies onto `config.web.publicBaseUrl`. `publicUrl(path)` joins origin + path with no trailing slash unless `path` supplies one. Precedence: `web.publicBaseUrl` → `GUEST_PHOTOBOOTH_URL` → file booth URL → LAN default. Rejects a LAN IP as a short-link target (`isUsableShortLinkOrigin`) |
| `src/tinyurl-credentials.js` | TinyURL API token — one store, optional per-scope overrides (`guestbook`, `guestsnaps`, `games`). Ladder: scope env `TINYURL_API_TOKEN_GAMES` → saved `overrides[scope]` → global env `TINYURL_API_TOKEN` → saved `token`. File `{ token, overrides, updatedAt }` is backward compatible. Settings → Global owns the shared token; feature cards may store an override. Env wins (**409**) |
| `src/guest-book-settings.js` | Guest Book settings in `data/guest-book-settings.json`: enable/pause, preferred alias, who-can-send, scrypt password, `rateLimitEnabled` plus rates, blocked words, approval, invite footer, guests-may-wake. Password hash is never returned |

Readings cached in memory for `refreshMinutes`; a failed fetch falls back to the stale map rather than a blank board. Two modes: `temperature` (six bands, white **coldest**) and `conditions` (WMO codes) |

| `src/word-scramble.js` | **Word Scramble** — find English words in a 4×4 grid. Classic 16 dice minus Q. A word counts when every letter is on the board and no tile is spent twice (letters do not have to touch). Binary-search lookup on a merged public-domain English list, quality gate (≥30 words including one 6-letter), `everyone` / `cancel` duplicate rules. `SCORE_LADDER` pays every extra letter more (3→1, 4→2, 5→4, 6→6, 7→9, 8→12, then +3 a letter) |
| `src/word-scramble-words.json` | Sorted merge of ENABLE1, 12dicts 2of12inf, and words_alpha (3–16 letters). Sixteen because every cell may be used. Rebuild with `tools/build-word-scramble-words.js` |
| `tools/build-word-scramble-words.js` | Fetch public-domain ENABLE1, 12dicts 2of12inf, and words_alpha; merge and write the shipped scramble corpus |
| `tools/build-game-decks.js` | Rebuild Party Prompts + Wheel of Fortune decks: keep the house lists, harvest everyday English idioms/proverbs from open MIT/CC0 lists (not a TV-show archive), add family-safe extras, and re-gate every row |
| `src/games/registry.js` | Game types (`scramble`, `prompts`, `wheel`, `hangman`). Each registers a Vestaboard `source` and must route its cards through `games/sessions.js`, which takes the board lock. `BOARD_SOURCES` / `GAME_BY_SOURCE` map both ways |
| `src/games/modes/*.js` | One file per game — everything `sessions.js` must **not** know. Contract: `id`, `title`, `source`, `minPlayers`, `votes`, `createRound`, `roundKey`, `seat`, `isSeated`, `submit`, `livePoints`, `closeRound`, `publicRound`, `boardExtras`, `archiveExtras`, plus `beginVoting` / `canVote` for games that vote and `roundHoldSeconds` / `beginRound` / `onRoundTimeout` / `onLeave` for turn-based games. `submit` / `onLeave` may return `advance` to skip the rest of the current phase clock. `scramble.js` is the 4×4 grid; `prompts.js` is write-then-vote (last locked answer starts voting); `wheel.js` is spin / letter / solve; `hangman.js` is set-the-word / letter / solve on six shared lives |
| `src/games/sessions.js` | Live sessions: 4-letter codes, `invited → lobby → round → intermission → … → final → closed`, SSE, 1s tick (`NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` skips it), idle close. `lastSseAt` refreshes on **join** and on every SSE connect/disconnect (including the last phone leaving), so an invite that sat on the board longer than `idleTimeoutSeconds` cannot die on the next tick after the first join. An empty invite's lobby window (`lobbySeconds`) starts when the invite **posts** to the board, not when Push queued it; until then only `inviteTtlMinutes` is a safety net. That lobby window is also the card's `holdSeconds` and the phone countdown. Cancelling a waiting invite closes the session and drops its queue page. First join starts a fresh lobby. Phase posts use `replaceSource: 'word.scramble'` + `replaceCard` (the phase name) + `gameSource` + `holdSeconds`; only the invite uses `breakHold` to take the board — later cards stay in front of held non-game pages and do not evict an earlier phase that has not flipped yet. `takeBoard()` drops only multi-page sequence tails (alerts survive; single-page scheduler items stay queued behind the game lock). Intermission posts a round-winner card; final posts high scores then best word as one sequence. Last player leaving, admin stop, idle, and invite expiry after someone sat down close on the **final scores** card (live standings at that moment) instead of a blank board; a no-show invite just drops the lock so rotation can continue. A normal finish already showing `final` is left up. `scores` is rebuilt on every read from banked points **plus the open round**. Names are title-cased and de-duplicated — a second Daddy joins as `Daddy (2)`. SSE is **per listener**: `you.words` is only that phone's list; `lastRound` reveals every word between rounds. **Game-agnostic**: the phase machine, codes, lobby, clock, board lock, archive and SSE live here; anything shaped like one game is delegated to its `games/modes/*` mode. `replaceSource` / `gameSource` come from `mode.source`. Modes with `votes: true` insert a **`voting`** phase between `round` and `intermission` (`votingSeconds`, skipped by `canVote` when fewer than two answers landed). A `minPlayers` floor (settings, else `mode.minPlayers`) is checked when the lobby breaks: too few and the session closes with reason `not-enough-players` on a **`short`** card that says how many were needed. `publicSession` carries `gameType`, `title`, `minPlayers`, `needPlayers` plus whatever `mode.publicRound` adds |
| `src/games/archive.js` | Month JSONL at `data/game-sessions/YYYY-MM.jsonl`, `sessionId` dedupe. Archived on finish and abandon. `remove(ids)` rewrites the month files without those rows (unparseable lines are kept, not swept) |
| `src/games/settings.js` | `data/game-settings.json`, namespaced by game id, re-read on every `get()`. Per-game sanitisers clamp their own keys. The short-link alias is **not** per game — `alias()` / `setAlias()` read and write the one value both game cards edit |
| `src/party-prompts.js` | **Party Prompts** — answer an open-ended prompt with your wittiest line, then vote for somebody else's. One vote is one point, so the leaderboard stays single-digit. Loads the shipped deck, gates prompts at three board rows and answers at three rows / 60 chars, folds answers to the board alphabet (so `Cup holder` and `CUP HOLDER` are one answer), and `scoreRound` ignores self-votes and second votes |
| `src/party-prompts-prompts.json` | 584 board-fit family-safe prompts (354 original house lines plus 230 new). Open-ended by construction — no yes/no openers, no question marks. Rebuild with `tools/build-game-decks.js` |
| `src/wheel-of-fortune.js` | **Wheel of Fortune** — spin, call a letter, solve. Classic living-room rules folded to 6×22: consonants pay wedge × count, vowels cost $250, BANKRUPT zeros the round bank, only the solver banks (\$1,000 floor), Y is a consonant. Loads the shipped deck and gates every puzzle at three board rows |
| `src/wheel-of-fortune-puzzles.json` | 984 family-safe board-fit puzzles (phrase / place / thing / food / person / around the house / on the map / living thing / event / what are you doing / movie / song / before and after). Phrases are everyday English idioms and proverbs harvested from open MIT/CC0 lists and gated to common words; other categories are real places, foods, events and public-domain songs. Rebuild with `tools/build-game-decks.js` |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/wheel-of-fortune.js` | Wheel of Fortune invite / lobby / puzzle / round winner / high scores / biggest win / not-enough-players. Yellow chips own the first and last two flaps; hidden letters are white chips so the board reads like the TV puzzle |
| `src/hangman.js` | **Hangman** — six shared lives, a right letter keeps your turn, a miss (or a wrong solve) costs a life and passes it. Loads the shipped deck, gates every word at one board row (4–14 letters, A–Z), masks, and owns the scoring: 10 a flap turned over, 50 + 5 a surviving life for the solve, 5 a forced miss to the setter (cap 30, **nil unless somebody solves it**). A word a player types is checked against the Word Scramble dictionary |
| `src/hangman-words.json` | 1,120 words in 19 categories (animals / food and drink / around the house / kitchen / sports / travel / nature / music / jobs / science / clothes / celebrations / body / feelings / movies / places / school / garden / adventure). The category is the only hint, so every word has to be guessable from it across the room; each one is also in the scramble dictionary, which is what keeps smashed-together phrases (`MEASURINGCUP`) out. Re-gate with `tools/_hangman-clean.js` |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/hangman.js` | Hangman invite / lobby / masked word / reveal / high scores / toughest word / not-enough-players. Orange chips own the first and last two flaps; on the round card the masthead splits into `HANGMAN` left and `#CODE` right so late join stays readable without costing a row (`titleRow(text, { code })`, which stands the code down rather than crowd a title too long to share); hidden letters are white chips, spaced a flap apart until the word is too long to afford it; the six lives are a `LIVES` row of chips draining green to red. The flap alphabet has no backslash, so the board never tries to draw the stick figure — the phone does that |
| `src/web/games/hangman.js` | Hangman on the phone: a tile per letter, the gallows drawn a limb at a time (six `data-at` limbs, plus life pips and the missed letters), a keypad that greys hits green and misses red, a solve box, and the setter's three-word menu (or their own word). Only the phone whose turn it is gets a keypad; the setter sees the word they chose. A miss shakes the gallows once (`prefers-reduced-motion` skips it) |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/party-prompts.js` | Party Prompts invite / lobby / prompt / voting / round winner / high scores / best answer / not-enough-players. Violet chips own the first and last two flaps and the title centres between them; every body line is centred and vertically centred in rows 2–4. The prompt card closes on `JOIN AT <link>` (bare link when the alias is too long) and carries `CODE ABCD` under the title while late joining is on. The winner title is the **short** `ROUND n WINNER` — `ROUND n OF m WINNER` is one flap too wide. Scores are name-dots-number, all rows one width; the code costs the fifth score, so the final card drops it |
| `src/vestaboard/formatters/games.js` | Word Scramble invite / lobby / 4×4 round / round-winner intermission / high scores / best word. Title centres between the green chips; lobby keeps the invite and fills the blank row with `N PLAYER(S)`; intermission shows `ROUND n OF m WINNER` + running total; final sequences scores then best word; round card carries `ROUND n OF m` and (while late joining is on) `CODE ABCD` |
| `src/web/games/` | One page for every game. `games.js` is the **shell**: join, SSE, clock, code line, standings, submit, and a `window.GameShell.register(id, handlers)` registry — it hands each session to the module whose id matches `session.gameType` and marks every `[data-game]` panel that belongs to another game with `data-off`. Join waits for that module (`whenGameReady` / `enterSession`) before flipping to `#gm-play`, and a refresh reconnects the seat stored in `sessionStorage` via `GET /api/games/session?sessionId=`. `?code=` + `?name=` auto-join waits for `window.load` so the modules below this file have registered. `scramble.js`, `party-prompts.js`, `wheel.js`, and `hangman.js` are the modules; a module supplies `render`, optional `scoreLine` / `phaseLabel`, and `teardown`. Below: what each module draws |
| `src/web/games/party-prompts.js` | Party Prompts on the phone: the prompt as the hero, one answer box with a live counter, and once you have sent it your answer replaces the box so nobody wonders whether it went through. Voting is a list of full-width thumb-height buttons carrying throwaway `answerId`s — **no names until the reveal** — with your own answer shown but disabled. The reveal puts the names back, winner first |
| `src/web/games/wheel.js` | Wheel of Fortune on the phone: the masked puzzle, a spinning SVG wheel every player watches (built from `session.wheel` once a round needs it — not on the lobby join — turned to `session.spin.index`), whose turn it is, a Spin button for that player only, a letter pad (consonants after a cash spin, vowels when the bank can pay $250), and a solve box. Called letters grey out |
| `src/web/games/scramble.js` | Public `/games/` — join by code + first name (the name is remembered in `localStorage` under `signal.games.name`), SSE, grid + word input. **Letters are buttons**: only tiles already in the current word grey out (`is-used`); tap the last one to undo; everything else stays live and validity is checked on submit. Typing lights up any unused tiles that spell the word (letters do not have to touch); an impossible letter shakes the compose bar, clears the attempt, and drops every tile pick. Phone first and responsive: one column with the compose bar stuck to the thumb line, board beside the word/score cards from 760px, a capped 620px stage centred against a 400px sidebar from 1080px, and one centred column again whenever there is no grid (lobby, intermission, final). Tiles size off their own container (`cqi`) and off `--gm-vh` / `--gm-board-max` (visual viewport, published by `scramble.js`). Focusing the word box on a phone or when the soft keyboard is up adds `body.gm-keyboard`: the page locks to the visual viewport and the 4×4 shrinks into a square at the top so every letter stays tappable. Tapping a tile while typing blurs the field and restores the large board; focusing the box again puts the caret after the last letter. Desktop / hardware-keyboard focus (`gm-typing` only) does not shrink the board |
| `src/word-riddles.js` | **Word Riddles** — pick a shipped or house-edited riddle+answer and build `word.riddles` (intro → riddle → reveal). Riddle and answer fill all six rows; the physical wordmark is not a flap. No network. Skips recent ids |
| `src/word-riddles-settings.js` | Hidden shipped ids, riddle/answer overrides, custom riddles, reveal delay, intro toggle, recent ids in `data/word-riddles-settings.json`. Settings → Game night |
| `src/word-riddles-riddles.json` | Shipped board-fit riddles (~575) from crawsome/riddles (Unlicense), nkilm/riddles-api (MIT), and public-domain classics. Rebuild with `tools/build-word-riddles.js` |
| `tools/build-word-riddles.js` | Fetch open riddle dumps and keep family-safe pairs that fit 6×22 (riddle) + 2×22 (answer) |
| `src/amazing-facts.js` | **Amazing Facts** — pick a shipped or house-edited quirk and build `amazing.facts`. No network. Skips recent ids; optional category pool |
| `src/amazing-facts-settings.js` | Hidden shipped ids, text overrides, custom facts, category pool, recent ids in `data/amazing-facts-settings.json`. Settings → News |
| `src/amazing-facts-facts.json` | Shipped amazing facts (~4.2k board-fit) from Royal-lobster science-facts-project (MIT). Rebuild with `tools/build-amazing-facts.js` |

| `src/conversation-starters.js` | **Conversation Starters** — pick a shipped or house-edited prompt and build `talk.starters`. No network. Skips recent ids |

| `src/baking-inspiration.js` | **Baking Inspiration** — pick a shipped or house-edited ≤5-ingredient idea and build `bake.inspire`. No network. Skips recent ids |

| `src/calendar-clock.js` | **Calendar Clock** — marketplace monthly calendar + house clock (no network). Seven chip columns, `SMTWTFS`/`MTWTFSS` only when the month fits in five week-rows, per-month colour with a contrasting today chip. Builds `calendar.clock`. Settings in `data/calendar-clock-settings.json` (week start; Settings → Global) |
| `src/word-clock.js` | **Word Clock** — the time spelled out (no network): `IT'S A QUARTER PAST TWELVE IN THE AFTERNOON.` Midnight and noon are named, not counted; multiples of five drop the word `MINUTES`. `layoutLines()` narrows the wrap until the block is roughly rectangular, then `wordClockRows()` centres it on both axes across all six rows. Builds `word.clock`; `statusSnapshot().boardRows` is what the admin bezel paints. Settings in `data/word-clock-settings.json` (`rounding` five/exact, `dayPart`; Settings → Global) |

Hand-set start/end is the full range (600s safety cap); automatic snippets stay 5s. Raw RGBA encode is only a short-clip fallback |

Source `.mp4` is never shipped |

| `src/huupe-adb.js` | Bridge-side ADB collector: finds `adb`, connects, identifies the hoop via `getprop`, streams `logcat`, retries with backoff. Heartbeats the live stream every 30s (`adb shell echo`) because a sleeping hoop drops the connection without closing it, and logcat silence is normal. **Does not sweep the LAN on a cold start** — discovery is an explicit Settings action; `autoDiscover` is only the recovery path when a known host stops answering |
