## Docker notes (QNAP)

- **`network_mode: host`** — required for UDP LAN + auth proxy on NAS IP
- **`./src:/app/src:ro`** — edit JS on host without image rebuild
- **`./data:/app/data`** — session + config persist across restarts
- Listener service name: `signal-bridge` (container/image: `signal-bridge`)
- Auth: `docker compose -p signal-auth -f docker-compose.auth.yml up --no-build`

---

## Commands

| Command | When |
|---------|------|
| `docker compose up -d` | Start listener |
| `./recreate.sh` | Restart listener (no build) |
| `PROXY_OWN_IP=x.x.x.x ./reauth.sh` | Re-authenticate Amazon |
| `docker compose logs -f` | Tail logs |
| `npm run diagnose` | Test session inside container |
| `npm run tesla-register` | Register `TESLA_FLEET_DOMAIN` with Tesla (run once; PEM must be hosted) |
| `npm run tesla-verify-register` | Confirm domain registration |
| `npm run tesla-auth` | Tesla OAuth on **PC** (`http://localhost:4381/callback`); saves `data/tesla-session.json` |
| `tesla-auth-pc.bat` | Same as npm tesla-auth (Windows; `pushd` for UNC NAS paths) |
| `./tesla-register.sh` | Register Fleet domain on NAS |
| `./tesla-verify-register.sh` | Verify Fleet registration |
| `./tesla-status.sh` | Tesla session / auth-status summary |
| `docker compose exec -it signal-bridge sh` | Interactive shell in listener container |

---

## Dependencies

- **Node ≥ 18**
- **`alexa-remote2`** (^8) — unofficial Alexa API; wraps `alexa-cookie2` for auth/refresh

---

## Control web page (`src/web-server.js` + `src/web/`)

Served by the listener at **`https://<NAS_IP or hostname>:47810/`** (config `webServer.{enabled,port,https}`; TLS in `data/web-certs/` — self-signed by default, or Let's Encrypt PEMs via `./issue-letsencrypt-cert.sh` → Certbot inside the container). Optional HTTP→HTTPS redirect on **47811**.

| URL | Who | What |
|-----|-----|------|
| `/` | Everyone | Landing page (`src/web/index.html` + `landing.css`) — household username/password (and Forgot password) above Games / Guest Snaps / Guest Book / Admin tiles. Signed-in visitors see Continue as {firstName}. The Guest Book tile only appears when `GET /api/guestbook/status` says it is on. Footer links to Privacy and Terms |
| `/privacy` | Everyone | Household privacy policy (`src/web/privacy.html`) — Google OAuth consent branding |
| `/terms` | Everyone | Household terms of use (`src/web/terms.html`) — Google OAuth consent branding |
| `/oauth-logo.png` | Everyone | 120×120 PNG app mark for the Google Cloud OAuth consent screen |
| `/user/` | Signed-in users | Household app: icon tab bar (Message / Skills / Game Sessions / Vestaboard Simulator + permissioned Flight Plan / Slideshow / Date Book), header **Signal** (back to `/`) + Profile dialog + Log out, Skills dashboard (HTML5 drag on desktop, long-press / grip on phones; search + kind + category chips with live counts, and a drag inside a filtered grid is folded back into the full tile order), Games auto-join, shared Vestaboard simulator (viewport-fit bezel; queue beside the board only from 1600px), Date Book (day-of message + theme picker with a Countdown / Day of preview) |
| `/guestsnaps/` | Guests | Photo booth — **6-digit PIN gate** (rotates every 24h; PIN shown on `guest.photobooth` overlay). After unlock: pick a display, queue one or more photos (camera or camera roll), then send — one photo is `qr.display` photo mode; two or more is `photo.slideshow` of just that queue |
| `/guestbook/` | Guests | Guest Book — leave a message for the Vestaboard |
| `/games/` | Guests | Game night — join with the code on the board. `?code=` + `?name=` prefill and auto-join after load (household Join now). A refresh reconnects a stored seat |
| `/admin/` | Host | Full SPA (Push / Remote / Control / Slideshow / Settings), gated by household admin session. Header **Signal** (back to `/`), **User management** dialog (user cards + editor sheet, set-password sheet for create/reset, Audit table); Settings → Accounts **Email** (Gmail OAuth sender). The env `ADMIN_USERNAME` account is visible but locked except name and picture |
| `/admin/login.html` | Host | Admin password form; **Back to Signal** returns to `/` |

Public APIs: `GET /api/displays` (+ events SSE), `GET /api/guest/session`, `POST /api/guest/login|logout|request-pin`, `GET /qr-images/*`. Photo upload/push require a **guest** (`signal_guest`) or **admin** session. Everything else requires an admin session. If `ADMIN_PASSWORD` is unset, admin APIs fail closed (503).

**Reverse-proxy subpaths:** the SPA uses a dynamic `<base href>` (from `location.pathname`) plus relative asset/API URLs, so a path-stripping proxy (e.g. public `/signal/` → bridge `/`) works without hardcoding a prefix. Prefer a trailing slash on the public mount URL.

**iPhone / Chrome QR:** open the **https** URL once, accept the certificate warning (Advanced → Proceed), then **Scan QR Code** uses the live camera (`getUserMedia` + jsQR). Plain HTTP cannot use the camera on iOS — put your NAS IP in `webServer.certHosts` (or `PROXY_OWN_IP`) before first cert generation, or delete `data/web-certs/` and restart after updating hosts.

**JSON API:**

| Route | Effect |
|-------|--------|
| `GET /api/displays` | Known displays from `display.announce` registry (`id` unique; `label` disambiguates duplicate names) |
| `GET /api/displays/events` | SSE stream — pushes `displays` events whenever the registry changes |
| `POST /api/displays/discover` | Broadcast `display.discover`, wait ~2.5s for re-announces, prune silent displays; returns `{ displays, removedIds }` |
| `POST /api/displays/auth/start` | Show 6-digit PIN on selected display (`display.auth`); required before mouse/keyboard/power |
| `POST /api/displays/auth/verify` | `{targetId,pin}` → `controlToken` session for that display |
| `POST /api/displays/auth/status` | Unlock / challenge status for a display |
| `GET /api/guest/session` | Guest booth auth status (`authenticated`, `expiresAt`, `pinDigits`) — never includes the PIN |
| `POST /api/guest/login` | `{pin}` → `signal_guest` cookie until the daily PIN expires; progressive IP lockout on failures |
| `POST /api/guest/logout` | Clear guest session cookie |
| `POST /api/guest/request-pin` | Push `guest.photobooth` (with current PIN) to all displays; phone response omits the PIN |
| `POST /api/push/tesla-dashboard` / `tesla-battery` | Synthetic event (`trigger: "web-api"`) through `listener.recordVoiceEvent`; body may include `targetId` |
| `POST /api/push/weather` / `shopping-list` | Synthetic voice-query event (`trigger: "web-api"`) through `listener.recordVoiceEvent`, same as if Alexa had been asked — cached weather/shopping data is served immediately |
| `GET`/`POST /api/locale/settings` | House pin (city, ZIP, °F/°C, base currency). POST geocodes via Zippopotam + Open-Meteo and writes `data/locale-settings.json` |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/public-url/settings` | Public base URL (`https://…`, no trailing slash). Writes `data/public-url-settings.json` and re-ensures the Guest Book short link when an alias is set. **400** if the URL is not https |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/tinyurl/settings` | Global TinyURL token. **409** when `TINYURL_API_TOKEN` is in the environment |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/guest-book/settings` | Guest Book settings + optional TinyURL override (`scope: guestbook`). Token save is **409** when global or `TINYURL_API_TOKEN_GUESTBOOK` env is set. Calls `ensure('guestbook', '/guestbook/')`. Never returns `passwordHash` |
| `POST /api/guest-book/check` | Health-check the Guest Book short link now (HEAD without redirect; repair ladder on mismatch) |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/guest-snaps/settings` | Guest Snaps preferred TinyURL alias + optional shared token. Calls `ensure('guestsnaps', '/guestsnaps/')` against the Public base URL. **409** if env token. |
| `POST /api/guest-snaps/check` | Health-check the Guest Snaps short link now |
| `GET /api/guestbook/status` | Public: enabled/paused/whoCanSend/charset plus `inviteFooter`, `editableRows`, `footerRows` (locked invite overlay). No PIN or password |
| `POST /api/guestbook/preview` | Public live 6×22 layout for the compose page |
| `POST /api/guestbook/send` | Public send. Rate-limited (**429** + `retryAfterSeconds` + `Retry-After`; wait copy only — no published cap). Pushes `guest.book` to the Vestaboard |
| `POST /api/guestbook/unlock` | Public password/board-code unlock → `signal_guestbook` cookie |
| `POST /api/push/guest-book-invite` | Vestaboard Guest Book Invite (`guestbook.invite` → `guest.book.invite`). Target coerced to a board. **409** when there is no short link yet |
| `GET /api/guest-book/book` | Admin: The Book list (`page`, `pageSize`, optional `status=waiting|released`). Also returns `waiting` count |
| `POST /api/guest-book/replay` / `release` / `delete` | Admin replay/push; **release** a `waiting` entry to the board (→ `released`); delete one or `ids[]`. Replay/release also accept `ids[]` and queue **oldest→newest**. Replay refuses `waiting` (409 — release first) |
| `POST /api/push/weekly-weather` | Vestaboard Weekly Weather Report from the house pin (`weather.weekly`); live Open-Meteo 7-day with `data/weather-cache.json` fallback. Target coerced to a board |
| `GET` / `POST /api/weather-alerts/settings` | Weather Alerts severity filters (min severity, watches/advisories, max pages). `POST { reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/weather-alerts` | Vestaboard Weather Alerts (`weather.alerts`) from NWS for the house pin. **400** without a pin |
| `GET` / `POST /api/stock-market/settings` | Stock Market watchlist (≤10 tickers), change mode, provider, optional Finnhub key. `POST { reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/stock-market` | Vestaboard Stock Market (`stocks.market`). Target coerced to a board. **502** if every quote fails |
| `GET` / `POST /api/currency-rates/settings` | World Currency Rates quote list (≤12 ISO codes). `POST { reset: true }` restores EUR/GBP/JPY/CAD/MXN/ARS/BSD/CNY. Base currency is locale |
| `POST /api/push/currency-rates` | Vestaboard World Currency Rates (`fx.rates`). Target coerced to a board. **502** if every rate fails |
| `GET` / `POST /api/iss-tracker/settings` | ISS tracker units + which lines to show (coords / altitude / daylight). `POST { reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/iss-tracker` | Vestaboard ISS tracker (`iss.track`). Target coerced to a board. **502** if position APIs fail |
| `GET` / `POST /api/starlink-tracker/settings` | Starlink look-ahead, min elevation, prefer-visible, weather/visibility lines. `POST { reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/starlink-tracker` | Vestaboard Starlink Tracker (`starlink.track`). **400** without a house pin; **502** if Satlas fails |
| `GET` / `POST /api/space-launch-alerts/settings` | Space Launch Alerts look-ahead, chip colour, cache refresh interval, suborbital toggle, launch list + preview. `POST { refresh: true }` refetches Launch Library 2; `POST { reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/space-launch-alerts` | Vestaboard Space Launch Alert (`launch.alert`). Optional `{ launchId }`; otherwise next upcoming launch. **409** if cache is empty |
| `GET`/`POST /api/learn-japanese/settings` | JLPT level + part-of-speech filters for Learn Japanese (`data/learn-japanese-settings.json`) |
| `POST /api/push/learn-japanese` | Vestaboard Learn Japanese card (`japanese.learn`) from the shipped romaji lexicon. Target coerced to a board. **409** if filters match nothing |
| `GET`/`POST /api/learn-{portuguese,spanish,french,german,italian}/settings` | CEFR A1/A2 + POS filters (`data/learn-{lang}-settings.json`) |
| `POST /api/push/learn-{portuguese,spanish,french,german,italian}` | Vestaboard Learn {Language} card. Target coerced to a board. **409** if filters match nothing |
| `POST /api/push/quiet-hours-reminder` | Vestaboard Quiet Hours Reminder (`quiet-hours.reminder`). Random night card. Target coerced to a board. Always quiet-hours exempt |
| `GET`/`POST /api/chuck-norris/facts` | Chuck Norris fact list (search/page) and add/edit/hide. Persist in `data/chuck-norris-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/chuck-norris` | Vestaboard Chuck Norris Fun Facts (`chuck.facts`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every fact is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/roast-me/roasts` | Roast list (search/page) and add/edit/hide/remove. Persist in `data/roast-me-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/roast-me` | Vestaboard Roast Me! (`roast.me`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every roast is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/family-quotes/quotes` | Family quote list (search by quote or author, page) and add/edit/hide/remove. Persist in `data/family-quotes-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/family-quotes` | Vestaboard Family Quotes (`family.quotes`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every quote is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/warm-fuzzies/fuzzies` | Warm fuzzy list (search/page) and add/edit/hide/remove. Persist in `data/warm-fuzzies-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/warm-fuzzies` | Vestaboard Warm Fuzzies (`warm.fuzzies`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every fuzzy is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/daily-bucket-fillers/fillers` | Daily Bucket Filler list (search/page) and add/edit/hide/remove. Persist in `data/daily-bucket-fillers-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/daily-bucket-fillers` | Vestaboard Daily Bucket Fillers (`bucket.fillers`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every filler is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/misheard-lyrics/lyrics` | Misheard lyric list (search by lyric or artist, page) and add/edit/hide/remove. Persist in `data/misheard-lyrics-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/misheard-lyrics` | Vestaboard Misheard Lyrics (`misheard.lyrics`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every lyric is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/us-state-facts/settings` | Census-region filter + recent rotation. `{reset:true}` clears filters. Preview rows for all 50 states |
| `POST /api/push/us-state-facts` | Vestaboard US State Facts (`state.facts`). Optional `{id}` / `{name}`; otherwise a random unused state. **409** if filters match nothing |
| `GET`/`POST /api/us-weather-map/settings` | Map mode (`temperature`/`conditions`), refresh window, legend and cache age. `{reset:true}` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/us-weather-map` | Vestaboard US Weather Map (`us.weather-map`). `{force:true}` skips the cache. **502** if the fetch fails with nothing cached, or the map comes back with holes |
| `GET`/`POST /api/dad-jokes/jokes` | Dad joke list (search by setup or punchline, page) and add/edit/hide/remove. Persist in `data/dad-jokes-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/dad-jokes` | Vestaboard Dad Jokes (`dad.jokes`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every joke is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/word-riddles/riddles` | Word Riddles list (search/page) and add/edit/hide. Persist in `data/word-riddles-settings.json` |
| `GET`/`POST /api/word-riddles/settings` | Reveal delay (10–180s) and intro-card toggle |
| `POST /api/push/word-riddles` | Vestaboard Word Riddles (`word.riddles`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every riddle is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/word-scramble/settings` | Word Scramble timings, duplicate rule, `allowLateJoin`, shared `/games/` alias, optional TinyURL override, `activeSession` |
| `GET /api/game-sessions` | Admin: live game sessions |
| `GET /api/game-sessions/history` | Admin: archived sessions, 10 per page |
| `POST /api/game-sessions/end` | Admin: end a live session (confirm sheet, never `window.confirm`) |
| `POST /api/push/word-scramble` | Create a Word Scramble invite and post it. Vestaboard only |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/party-prompts/settings` | Party Prompts timings, min/max players, `allowLateJoin`; shared `/games/` alias + TinyURL override; `activeSession` |
| `POST /api/push/party-prompts` | Create a Party Prompts invite and post it. Vestaboard only |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/wheel-of-fortune/settings` | Wheel of Fortune timings (`turnSeconds` + puzzle `roundSeconds`), min/max players, `allowLateJoin`; shared `/games/` alias + TinyURL override; `activeSession` |
| `POST /api/push/wheel-of-fortune` | Create a Wheel of Fortune invite and post it. Vestaboard only |
| `GET`/`POST`/`PUT /api/hangman/settings` | Hangman timings (`turnSeconds`, `pickSeconds` for the setter, word `roundSeconds`), min/max players, `allowLateJoin`, `wordSetter`, `categoryId`; also reports `wordCount`, `lives`, the category list, the shared `/games/` alias + TinyURL override, and `activeSession` |
| `POST /api/push/hangman` | Create a Hangman invite and post it. Vestaboard only |
| `POST /api/game-sessions/end` | End a live session — takes `sessionId` or `sessionIds[]`. Blanks the board so a dead game stops advertising itself |
| `POST /api/game-sessions/history/delete` | Forget archived games — `sessionId` or `sessionIds[]`; returns `{ removed }` |
| `GET /api/games/session` | Public: resolve a game by `code` or reconnect by `sessionId` (+ optional `playerId` for `you`) |
| `POST /api/games/join` | Public: seat a player, set `signal_games` cookie |
| `GET /api/games/events` | Public SSE (`: connected`, 15s `: ping`) |
| `POST /api/games/submit` | Public `{ action, payload }` (cookie-seated players only) |
| `POST /api/games/leave` | Public leave |
| `GET`/`POST /api/amazing-facts/facts` | Amazing Facts list (search/page/category), add/edit/hide, or save category push pool. Persist in `data/amazing-facts-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/amazing-facts` | Vestaboard Amazing Facts (`amazing.facts`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every fact is hidden / filtered out |
| `GET`/`POST /api/world-geography-facts/facts` | World Geography Facts list (search/page/category), add/edit/hide, or save category push pool. Persist in `data/world-geography-facts-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/world-geography-facts` | Vestaboard World Geography Facts (`geo.facts`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every fact is hidden / filtered out |
| `GET`/`POST /api/conversation-starters/prompts` | Conversation Starters list (search/page) and add/edit/hide. Persist in `data/conversation-starters-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/conversation-starters` | Vestaboard Conversation Starters (`talk.starters`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every prompt is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/stoic-quotes/quotes` | Stoic Quotes list (search/page) and add/edit/hide. Persist in `data/stoic-quotes-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/stoic-quotes` | Vestaboard Stoic Quotes (`stoic.quotes`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every quote is hidden |
| `GET`/`POST /api/on-this-day/events` | On This Day list (search/page/date filter), add/edit/hide, or save min/max year. Persist in `data/on-this-day-settings.json` |
| `POST /api/push/on-this-day` | Vestaboard On This Day (`history.day`). Optional `{ month, day }`. Target coerced to a board. **409** if nothing ready for that date |
| `GET` / `POST /api/baking-inspiration/ideas` | List / add / edit / hide Baking Inspiration ideas |
| `POST /api/push/baking-inspiration` | Vestaboard Baking Inspiration (`bake.inspire`). Target coerced to a board. **409** if every idea is hidden |
| `GET` / `POST /api/world-population/settings` | World Population baseline, annual births/deaths, footer label. `POST { reset: true }` restores UN defaults |
| `POST /api/push/world-population` | Vestaboard World Population Tracker (`world.population`). Target coerced to a board |
| `GET` / `POST /api/calendar-clock/settings` | Calendar Clock week start (`sunday` / `monday`). `POST { reset: true }` restores Sunday |
| `POST /api/push/calendar-clock` | Vestaboard Calendar Clock (`calendar.clock`). Target coerced to a board |
| `GET` / `POST /api/word-clock/settings` | Word Clock reading (`rounding` `five`/`exact`, `dayPart` bool) plus `payload` and `boardRows` for the preview. `POST { reset: true }` restores the defaults |
| `POST /api/push/word-clock` | Vestaboard Word Clock (`word.clock`). Target coerced to a board |
| `GET` / `POST` / `PUT /api/red-letter/settings` | Red Letter push vs schedule selection (`next` / `random`) and `showTime`. Also returns the Date Book with each event's next occurrence and `boardPreview` (the card Push Now would send). `{ reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `GET` / `POST /api/date-book/events` | List / add Date Book events (`name`, `message`, `date` or weekday rule, `time`, `recurring`, `layout`). **400** without a name and a date or weekday-of-month. A `PUT` that omits `layout` keeps the saved artwork; `layout: null` clears it |
| `PUT` / `DELETE /api/date-book/events/:id` | Patch one event (including `layout` — `{ cells }` to save artwork, `null` to remove it) or delete it. **404** for an unknown id |
| `POST /api/date-book/preview` | Both cards for `{ eventId }` or an unsaved `{ event }` — `{ countdown, dayOf }`, each with server-built `rows`. The admin previews and the designer render these, so the sheet cannot drift from the board |
| `POST /api/push/red-letter` | Vestaboard Red Letter (`redletter.show` → `red-letter.card`). Target coerced to a board. **409** when nothing in the Date Book is still ahead |
| `GET|POST|PUT /api/plex-top10/settings` | Plex Top 10 source / genres / cache minutes, plus the library's genre list for the picker. `{ reset: true }` restores defaults |
| `POST /api/push/plex-top10` | Vestaboard Plex Top 10 Movies (`plex.top10`). Target coerced to a board. **409** with no token or when nothing matches the genres; **502** when Plex is unreachable |
| `POST /api/push/air-quality` | Synthetic `air-quality` event (`show indoor air quality`) → multi-monitor indoor AQ overlay |
| `POST /api/push/indoor-temperature` | Synthetic `indoor-temperature` event for the first configured indoor sensor (or "temperature inside") |
| `POST /api/push/guest-photobooth` | Guest Snaps dual-QR welcome + booth access PIN on **all displays**; **503** if Wi‑Fi/booth URL not configured |
| `POST /api/push/timers` | Calls `listener.requestTimerPoll()` for an immediate Amazon notifications poll → UDP `timer.snapshot`; **503** if the hook isn't wired (older listener) |
| `POST /api/push/alarms` | Calls `listener.requestAlarmPoll()` for an immediate Amazon alarms poll → UDP alarm snapshot; **503** if the hook isn't wired |
| `POST /api/push/url` `{url,targetId?}` | Validate → UDP `web.open` (unicast when one display selected) |
| `POST /api/push/close-browser` | UDP `web.close` |
| `POST /api/qr/push` `{mode:"url"\|"wifi"\|"photo", url\|photos[]\|(ssid,password,security,hidden), label?, targetId?}` | URL/Wi-Fi → UDP `qr.display`. Photo mode: one URL → `qr.display` (`qrType:"photo"`); two or more (`photos`/`urls`, max 20) → `photo.slideshow` in **queued** order (not the whole camera roll) |
| `POST /api/qr/image-upload` `{imageDataUrl}` | Store a base64 photo (`qr-image-cache.js`, kept indefinitely) → `{path,token,createdAt}`; resolve `path` against `document.baseURI` client-side, then push through `/api/qr/push` (`mode:"photo"`) |
| `GET /qr-images/<token>.<ext>` | Serves a stored photo — 404s only if the token is unknown or was deleted |
| `GET /api/photos` | Lists every stored photo (newest-first) with `{token,path,createdAt}`, for the Slideshow Manager tab's camera roll and the "Shared Photo Slideshow" tile preview/count; also the manual-refresh fallback for `/api/photos/events` |
| `GET /api/photos/events` | SSE stream (same pattern as `/api/displays/events`) — pushes a `photos` event with `{reason,photos}` on connect (`reason:"hello"`) and again on every `store()`/`delete()` from *any* browser tab, so every open Slideshow Manager tab's camera roll stays live without polling |
| `POST /api/photos/delete` `{token}` or `{tokens:[]}` | Deletes one or more photos from the cache (Slideshow Manager tab, single or multi-select) → `{deleted:[],failed:[]}` |
| `GET /api/slideshow/settings` | Current Shared Photo Slideshow playback order (`{order,orders}`) |
| `GET`/`POST /api/slideshow/settings` | Read/update `{order, secondsPerPhoto}` in `data/slideshow-settings.json` (Settings tab — order segmented control + 5–60s time-per-photo slider) |
| `POST /api/push/photo-slideshow` `{photos:[{url,uploadedAt}],targetId?,order?}` | Builds UDP `photo.slideshow` from the given photos, reordered per the persisted slideshow setting unless `order:"queued"` keeps send order; 404→400 with a friendly message when there are no photos |
| `GET /api/vestaboards` | Board list for the Settings tab — per-board config plus live `health` and `hasKey`, never the key itself; also `house` (`dwellSeconds`, `priorities`) and `priorityCatalog` |
| `POST /api/vestaboards` | Add or edit a board (`{id,name,baseUrl,key?,quietHours}`); omitting `key` keeps the stored one. Applies live |
| `POST /api/vestaboards/house` `{dwellSeconds?,priorities?}` | Save house dwell and/or priorities. Omitted fields stay as they are |
| `POST /api/vestaboards/remove` `{id}` | Delete a board and its key. The simulator refuses — switch it off instead |
| `POST /api/vestaboards/enable` `{id,enabled}` | Start or stop that board's queue with no restart; a disabled board leaves the picker |
| `POST /api/vestaboards/test-flip` `{id}` | Queue the identity frame as an alert so it lands now |
| `POST /api/vestaboards/release-holds` `{id?}` | Drop active holds on every board (or one when `id` is set). Simulator Board tab **Release Holds** uses the house-wide form |
| `GET /api/vestaboard-sim` | One-shot state for the Simulator tab: board face, online flag, rate cooldown (max of flap window, Settings dwell, and live-game phase hold), `gameLock`, recent calls, queue + `queueRevision`, plus the encoder's code→glyph and chip tables so the page owns no character knowledge. 404 when the simulator is disabled in config |
| `GET /api/vestaboard-sim/events` | SSE stream — `sim.state` on connect and on every change, `sim.flip` (layout + transition strategy) after each accepted post, `sim.call` per request, `sim.queue` (`items` + `revision`) on connect and on every queue change. Never carries the board key |
| `POST /api/vestaboard-sim/online` `{online}` | Live on/off with no restart. Off makes the board answer 503, which is how retry/backoff and health reporting get exercised on purpose |
| `POST /api/vestaboard-sim/queue/cancel` `{id}` | Drop one waiting page. Already-gone ids still return 200 with the current queue (no toast) |
| `POST /api/vestaboard-sim/queue/reorder` `{ids}` | Put waiting pages in this order (drag-and-drop on the Board tab) |
| `POST /api/vestaboard-sim/queue/clear` | Drop every waiting page. What is already showing stays. `dropped` is how many went. Empty queue is 200 / `dropped: 0` |
| `POST /api/system/reboot` / `poweroff` | UDP `system.command` |
| `POST /api/input/pointer` / `key` | Relative mouse / key injection — **requires** a single `targetId` (not All) |
| `POST /api/input/text` `{value,pressEnter?,targetId?}` | Full-string keyboard input (logins/passwords/URLs) — UDP `input.text`; same PIN/`controlToken` gate as pointer/key |
| `POST /api/auth/tesla/start` | Returns Tesla authorize URL + opens one-shot local callback (default `http://0.0.0.0:4381`, 10-min timeout) → `saveTokensFromCode`. Public `TESLA_REDIRECT_URI` (e.g. `https://fleetapi…/callback`) must be proxied from the Fleet domain host to that listen port |
| `POST /api/auth/alexa/start` | Runs vendored login proxy in-process (`runAuth({exitOnComplete:false, overrides:{proxyOwnIp}})`, port 3456; `proxyOwnIp` derived from request Host); on success the bridge saves the session and **exits 0** so Docker `restart: unless-stopped` brings it back fresh |
| `GET /api/status` | Alexa/Tesla auth state, active pushed URL, uptime |

QR scanning (reading a code with the phone) is client-side: `<input type="file" capture>` photo → jsQR decode → confirm sheet → `POST /api/push/url`. `installAuthProxyPatch()` now runs at the top of `index.js` (before `alexa-remote2` loads) so the in-process login proxy works in listener mode.

**Push tab** — a search box and six segmented sub-tabs above the grid, the same shape as the Settings head: **Home** (Alexa's weather, **Weekly Weather Report** (Vestaboard), list, timers, alarms, notifications, air quality), **Games** (Steam, PSN, Autodarts, Huupe, Trivia, Roll Credits), **Media** (slideshow, Alexa Now Playing, YouTube, Feature Presentation, **Plex Top 10 Movies** (Vestaboard)), **News** (Upside News, Wikipedia), **Language** (Learn Japanese / Portuguese / Spanish / French / German / Italian — Vestaboard), **Travel** (Tesla, Overhead, Flight Plan), **Share** (Guest Snaps plus the hand-built Web Browser and QR Code cards). Tiles come from `GET /api/commands` and file themselves by `pushCategory`, so adding a command puts it in a pane without touching the markup — `assertValid()` refuses a pushable command with no category. Searching matches a tile's title, subtitle, service and command id (so "flightplan" finds Trip Board) and the static cards' labels and placeholders; each tab shows a hit count, tabs with no hits step aside during a search, and the pane switches itself to the first tab that still has something. A tab also hides when the selected display cannot show anything in it — picking a Vestaboard empties whole categories — and if that empties the page, the hint says whether the search or the display is responsible. **Home** is always the opening pane on load and after login; the sub-tab survives bottom-nav hops within one page load only (not `localStorage`). The query is not restored. Cache-bust `?v=signal105`.

**QR generator** (Push tab, separate from scanning above) lets the phone display a QR code on the target screen, mode tabs ordered **Photo | URL | Wi-Fi**: **Photo** (queue one or more JPEGs — camera roll multi-select or add-another after a camera shot — upload each to `/api/qr/image-upload`, then `/api/qr/push` `mode:"photo"`; one photo is a hero QR, two or more is a slideshow of just that queue), **URL** (plain link), or **Wi-Fi** (SSID/password → standard `WIFI:T:...;;` string, escaped via `buildWifiQrContent`). The bridge never renders a bitmap — it only ships the content string in `qr.display`; the display client generates the QR image locally.

**Display picker** — **All Displays** is always first and the default selection on load. When the Vestaboard Simulator is enabled it is listed next, then other Windows clients and boards (A–Z). A board option shows a health suffix when it is not `ok` (`key refused` / `not answering` / `off`). Selecting a board hides Control and Remote (those are UDP-only) and filters the Push grid to board-capable commands. A Vestaboard-capable push still fans out to every enabled board. All Displays still lists every tile. Scheduler rules have a **Show on** picker (`full` / `all` / `vestaboard` / one display); a board id still lands on the house line. Cache-bust `?v=signal101`.

**Settings tab, Vestaboards section** — the board list with live health (`OK` / `Key refused` / `Not answering` / `Off`), a **Test flip** button per board, switch on/off, **Edit** (including the simulator — address/key stay locked), and remove for real boards. Each row has a **Quiet Hours Reminder** checkbox (`quietHours.remindOnStart`). The form takes name, address, Local API key (write-only — an existing key survives an edit that leaves it blank), dwell, quiet-hours window, and the remind-on-start flag. A **Push Quiet Hours Reminder** button posts a test night card. Everything applies live.

**Vestaboard Simulator tab** — the Vestaboard as it would look on the wall. Every proportion and tone in `.vb-bezel` / `.vb-tile` was measured off a photograph of the physical Flagship rather than guessed, so the two easy mistakes are already corrected: the raised rails between columns are *lighter* than the flap faces they separate, and a flap window fills only the top ~60% of its cell — the band beneath it is open bezel, not part of the card. A 1.87:1 slab, a 7% bezel, a 27.7×49.6 cell holding a 21×30 window in a hard-edged recess, a light grotesque condensed to `scaleX(0.82)` sitting high in the window, a raking-light overlay, and the wordmark on the bottom bezel. The board fills the column (`width: 100%` — auto side margins would make it a shrink-to-fit flex item, and `contain: inline-size` leaves it nothing to shrink to, so it collapses to its padding), and `body[data-tab="board"]` widens `.content` to 1180/1460px because 22 columns want more room than a stack of settings cards. Colour chips are painted cards: same recess, same seam, only the face changes. A changed module walks the drum (`GET /api/vestaboard-sim` `drum`) in ~100ms top-hinged clicks, staggered so a full rewrite lasts as long as `vb-flip.wav` (~5.6s). The Board tab plays that clip on a drum walk (Web Audio clicks are fallback; muted with **Sound off**, skipped when `prefers-reduced-motion`). Toggling the board off dims the bezel. The rate pill says **Holding** while a lane lock owns the board (not “Waiting on game”). Below it, the pending **hub** queue (pointer-drag to reorder, × to cancel a page, **Clear queue** when anything is waiting, **Release Holds** to drop holds on every board in the house) and the last 20 API calls with their outcomes (`200 flipped` / `200 duplicate` / `400` / `401 auth bad` / `503 rate` / `503 offline`). The list follows `sim.queue` SSE plus a 2s poll while the tab is visible; a stale cancel just paints the current queue. The page boots `GET /api/vestaboard-sim` plus the SSE stream on load (not only when the tab is clicked) and holds no opinion about what the board should show.

**Slideshow tab** — camera-roll manager for everything in the QR image cache: thumbnail grid loads compact `/qr-images/thumbs/…` JPEGs (full originals only for the lightbox / display slideshow; missing thumbs fall back until backfill finishes), kept live via `GET /api/photos` + `GET /api/photos/events` SSE plus a manual refresh icon button; tap to open a lightbox with the upload date and a Delete button (left/right arrow buttons + ←/→ keyboard keys + swipe on touch screens to step between photos, with a "Photo x of y" counter), "Select" mode for multi-select + a "Select All"/"Unselect All" toggle + bulk delete, all deletes behind a themed confirm sheet (`POST /api/photos/delete`). The grid refreshes automatically when a photo is uploaded or deleted from any browser tab/session — no manual refresh needed unless the SSE connection is blocked/dropped. Settings tab gains a "Playback order" segmented control (Newest first / Oldest first / Shuffle) and a "Time per photo" slider (5–60s) that persist to `data/slideshow-settings.json` via `GET`/`POST /api/slideshow/settings` and are applied by the bridge whenever `/api/push/photo-slideshow` builds the UDP payload.

---

## Recent changes

- 2026-09-02: **Manual Push stamps the signed-in user on voice-style skills** — Indoor Air Quality (and other `/api/push/*` paths that go through `recordVoiceEvent`) lost `requestActor` when the HTTP request finished before Alexa enrich posted the board card, so the queue said `by System`. The actor is now captured on the synthetic voice event and forwarded on fan-out; timers/alarms carry it through their poll hooks the same way. Real Alexa / Autodarts / Huupe / YouTube / Steam / PSN detections still resolve to System; scheduler stays Scheduled. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **Doorbell cutting through a game releases the hold** — Hangman (Hold) + Red Letter waiting + Ring Doorbell (Now, no Hold) used to leave the game lock alive under RING DOOR BELL, so Red Letter stayed `held` and the simulator said Holding. A non-holding jumper that outranks the lock now ends it, drops that game's pending pages, emits `lock-preempted` (session closes without a FINAL SCORES re-take), and lets the queue continue. Sequenced pages also floor their gap at house dwell — formatter 15s stamps were flipping multi-page runs faster than Settings → Dwell. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `game-sessions`.
- 2026-09-02: **Household profile no longer tricks Chrome into Save password?** — the closed Profile sheet kept `pf-first` filled with `Admin` next to `type=password` fields marked `autocomplete="current-password"`, so after Skills / Simulator work Chrome offered to save a login nobody typed. Password inputs stay `disabled` until Profile opens, drop the login autocomplete tokens, and use non-login `name`s. Cache-bust user `signal288`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **First join no longer ends a long-waiting Hangman invite** — idle close armed on `hadPlayer && sseCount === 0` using `lastSseAt` from invite-create. An invite that sat ≥ `idleTimeoutSeconds` (Hangman default 180s) then died on the next tick after the first phone joined — Vestaboard `FINAL SCORES` with that player at 0, `/games/` blank or stuck, nobody else could join. Join now stamps `lastSseAt`, and the last SSE disconnect does too. The phone bounces back to the join form if the session vanishes under the EventSource. Cache-bust games `games.js?v=11`. Tests: `game-sessions`.
- 2026-09-02: **Games join no longer lands on a blank play shell** — entering a Wheel (or any game) code could flip to `#gm-play` before that game's module had registered, so every panel stayed `hidden` and three browsers looked empty until a refresh. Join now waits for the module (`enterSession` / `whenGameReady`), refuses to leave the form without a real session, auto-join waits for `window.load`, and a refresh reconnects the seat via `GET /api/games/session?sessionId=`. The wheel bitmap is built only once a round needs it, not on every lobby paint. Cache-bust games `games.js?v=10` / `wheel.js?v=4`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **Deeper Party Prompts and Wheel decks** — Party Prompts is 584 board-fit openers (230 new house lines). Wheel of Fortune is 984 puzzles: the house list minus a few too-short or duplicate rows, 360 everyday idioms/proverbs from open MIT/CC0 lists (common-word gated, not a TV-show archive), and 249 extra places / foods / events / public-domain songs / before-and-afters. Rebuild with `tools/build-game-decks.js`. Tests: `party-prompts`, `wheel-of-fortune`.
- 2026-09-02: **Game settings cards match Word Scramble** — Hangman, Party Prompts and Wheel of Fortune now have the same preferred-alias + TinyURL override + clear-override fields (they still edit the one `/games/` link). Titles are `<game> Settings`. The header pill is no longer the short-link health (`Unknown` when TinyURL had not been checked); it is whether that game has a live session (`Idle`, or `Lobby · ABCD` / `Live · ABCD`), polled every 5s. Play/timing grids were restacked so checkboxes no longer sit in a leftover fourth column. Cache-bust `signal287`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **New default avatars (16 sticker faces)** — the flat 14-icon set is replaced by soft sticker artwork on gradient tiles (cat ×2, bunny, raccoon, panda, red panda, fox, bear, polar bear, mouse, hamster, frog, owl, chick, koala, penguin), 200×200 and self-contained. Two things this touched beyond dropping files in: `sanitiseAvatar` **falls back** on an unknown id, so without `LEGACY_AVATAR_IDS` every household member on an old id would have come back as the first template — each retired id now maps to its nearest new face (the two otters, which have no successor, go to the tiles that kept their colour). And the artwork draws its own corners at 23%, so the CSS radii that used to round these images were clipping them a second time; `--avatar-radius` is now one ratio shared by both pickers, both previews and the header, and it rounds an uploaded photo the same way. Default is `cat-blue`. New tests pin the template list to the files on disk in both directions. Tests: `house-users`, `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **Your tiles gets the library's category chips, and a filtered drag stops eating tiles** — the household Skills tab could only be narrowed by search and by kind, so a long tile list had no way to say "just the games". The category chips now sit beside the Applies-to segment in the empty run that was already there, with live counts, and a category nothing matches steps aside (the one in force stays, or there would be no way out of it). `renderCatChips` / `countByCategory` are shared with the Skill library so the two lists cannot drift apart. **Bug found on the way:** `commitDashOrder` saved the grid as it stood, so dragging a tile while a search was active silently dropped every tile the search had hidden — `mergedDashOrder` now deals the visible tiles back into the slots they held and leaves the hidden ones where they were. Cache-bust `signal285`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **The Hangman join code rides the round card's masthead** — `showCode` was already on through a round (it follows `allowLateJoin`), but the code sat last in the footer's fallback chain behind `X TO GUESS`, which is always set, so nobody in the room ever saw it: the way in was only on the invite card, gone the moment play started. The masthead now splits into `HANGMAN` left and `#19UT` right, which costs the game none of the five rows it needs for the hint, the mask, the misses, the lives and the turn. A title too long to share the band (18 flaps between the chips) keeps it and drops the code. The `CODE` footer fallback is gone, and the pick card's code row with it. Tests: `vestaboard-hangman`.
- 2026-09-02: **Hangman's `holidays` deck is labelled CELEBRATIONS** — the hint read `HOLIDAYS` over words like SPARKLER, CONFETTI and PINATA, so a correct pick looked like a category mix-up (the hint and the word can't desync: `createRound` returns one row carrying both). Label only; the `id` stays `holidays` so a saved category filter survives. Golden frame updated in `vestaboard-hangman`.
- 2026-09-02: **Solo Hangman no longer shouts TIMES UP while the clock is still running** — a one-player turn timeout used to pass the turn to the same phone, stamp `TIMES UP` on the board, then start a fresh turn clock. The footer sat on that line for the whole next turn, so the wall said the game was over while the phone and the simulator still had seconds left. Alone, a quiet clock now just renews. With company the pass still happens, but the board footer prefers `X TO GUESS` over `TIMES UP`. Tests: `hangman-session`, `vestaboard-hangman`.
- 2026-09-02: **Game invites wait for the board, and Push stamps the user** — pushing Hangman (or any game) while another page was still dwelling started the lobby countdown at Push, so by the time the invite flipped the session was already closed: `/games/` rejected the code and Sessions was empty, with a dead "JOIN THE NEXT GAME" on the wall. The lobby window now starts when the invite card actually posts (`noteBoardShown` from the queue `posted` event); until then only the longer invite TTL is a safety net. Cancelling or ending an unshown invite drops its waiting queue page and closes the session (`invite-cancelled`). Game `pushBoard` also forwards `requestActor`, so the queue says "by Admin" instead of "by System". Tests: `game-sessions`, `vestaboard-queue`, `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **User-facing docs caught up** — `README.md` had stopped at late July: no Vestaboard, no household accounts, no games, no Now Playing integrations, no scheduler. It now covers both display kinds, the house queue (dwell / jump / Now / hold / quiet hours) and the simulator, household accounts and permissions, the four games, guests and short links, the Display Scheduler, and the **76-page skills catalog** (44 board-only) grouped by Push category — the counts come from `command-registry.js`, so re-run `node -e "…COMMANDS.length"` when they change. `DOCKER.md` gained the board ports (`:7000` simulator and hardware Local API), the integration-key block with the env-wins-409 rule, and board / login troubleshooting rows. `.env.example` gained TinyURL (global + per-scope), Vestaboard simulator binding, PSN, YouTube, trivia, Huupe ADB, timezone, contact and `yt-dlp` entries. The client README lists the panels it actually paints. Docs only — no code, no tests.
- 2026-09-02: **Hangman (fourth game)** — same session shell as the other three: 4-letter codes, per-turn clock, word wall-clock, late join sits out the current word, board lock `hangman.game`. **One player is enough** — alone the house deals the word; from two up, one phone sets it (three suggestions from three categories, or type your own, dictionary-checked) and everybody else takes turns, with that seat rotating each round. Six shared lives: a right letter keeps your turn, a miss or a wrong solve costs a life and passes it, a quiet clock costs only the turn. Scoring is 10 a flap turned over, 50 + 5 a surviving life for the solve, and 5 a forced miss to the setter (cap 30) **only if somebody solves it** — which is what stops anyone picking an unguessable word. A pick nobody makes (or a setter who walks off) falls to the house rather than leaving a blank board. 1,120 words in 19 categories, every one a single dictionary word that fits one board row. Board cards use orange chips, white chips for hidden letters and a green-to-red `LIVES` row; the gallows is drawn on the phone, because the flap alphabet has no backslash. Command `hangman.invite`, route `/api/push/hangman`, settings at `/api/hangman/settings`. Cache-bust admin `signal286` / games `hangman.js?v=1` / `games.css?v=12`. Tests: `hangman`, `hangman-session`, `vestaboard-hangman`, `command-registry`, `vestaboard-holds`, `vestaboard-priorities`, `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **The wheel is laid out like the show's, and spins as a bitmap** — 24 wedges instead of 20: $500-$900 around one $2,500 top dollar (which sits beside a BANKRUPT, like the sequined wedge), the two BANKRUPTs facing each other across the hub, LOSE A TURN and FREE SPIN between them. No two penalties touch and no value repeats next door — the old wheel sorted its cash and stacked BANKRUPT / BANKRUPT / LOSE A TURN / FREE SPIN in a block. `/games/` now paints the wheel once into a data-URI `<img>` so a throw is the compositor turning a decoded bitmap; as live SVG a phone re-rasterised two dozen paths and their rotated text every frame, which is why the spin stuttered on a phone and looked fine on a desktop. The turn is laid out before the wheel moves (no reflow mid-throw) and the wheel stops off-centre on an offset derived from the throw id, so every phone stops on the same picture. Cache-bust games `wheel.js?v=3` / `games.css?v=11`. Tests: `wheel-of-fortune`, `wheel-of-fortune-session`.
- 2026-09-02: **Admin reset-password fields stay hidden** — `#hu-pw` is `type="password"` like confirm (it was clear text). Each field has a Show/Hide control; create/reset stay masked until Show, generate still fills both, and the email-fallback reveal pane still shows the one password so it can be copied. Cache-bust admin `styles.css` / `app.js` `signal285` / `house-users.js` `signal267`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-02: **House queue: System byline, one Tesla card, cancel works** — Alexa/system pushes now stamp `actor.kind: system` / `name: System` so the queue reads "by System" instead of a blank line. Tesla battery (and dashboard) coalesce on one key, so the cached preview and the live Fleet reading replace rather than stacking four identical pages. Cancel only starts a drag from the grip; a row-wide pointerdown was swallowing the × click. Household users can cancel. Cache-bust `vestaboard-sim-ui` / admin `app.js` `signal284`. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `vestaboard-holds`, `vestaboard-router`, `vestaboard-sim-api`, `web-server`.
- 2026-09-01: **Admin dialogs centre on vertical room, not orientation** — sheet centring was gated on `(min-width: 640px) and (orientation: landscape)`, so two browser windows side by side on a landscape monitor (each *portrait* to CSS, and taller than any phone) fell back to the mobile bottom-sheet layout: Priorities opened past the halfway line with the top of the window empty. The gate is now `(min-width: 640px) and (min-height: 600px)`; a phone or a short window still gets the edge-anchored sheet. Priorities also grew its ceiling (`min(88dvh, 960px)`, picker `min(46dvh, 440px)`) so a tall window shows more of the order. Cache-bust admin `signal283`. Tests: `web-server`, `settings-filter`.
- 2026-09-01: **A game seat belongs to the tab, not the browser** — `signal_games` is one cookie per browser, so two people playing from one laptop (two windows) collapsed onto a single seat: both screens showed the same turn and the same Spin button, and the turn that passed to the seat nobody was watching sat there until it timed out. `/games/` now keeps its seat in `sessionStorage` and names it on join (`newSeat`), submit, leave and the SSE URL; `web-server.js` honours a seat that really is in the session it names and falls back to the cookie. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-01: **The wheel spins on the phone** — `wheelLayout()` ships the 20 wedges and `spinWheel` returns the `index` it landed on, so every phone paints one responsive SVG wheel and turns it to the wedge the server picked (`state.spin.id` marks a throw so a repaint never re-spins). The controls are held until the wheel settles, or the phone would spoil its own result; `prefers-reduced-motion` snaps instead. A turn still ends the instant the throw resolves — the next player gets a fresh clock, and an out-of-turn action is refused. Tests: `wheel-of-fortune`, `wheel-of-fortune-session`, `web-server`.
- 2026-09-01: **Priorities catalog hides phase cards** — Add-an-event no longer lists formatter aliases (`party.prompts.lobby`, `wheel.fortune.round`, `credits.show`, …). Those are cards of one event; the picker keeps the friendly Push title (Party Prompts, Wheel of Fortune, Roll Credits). Tests: `vestaboard-priorities`.
- 2026-09-01: **Party Prompts skip the write clock** — once every seated player has locked an answer, the session advances into voting immediately (`submit.advance` / last holdout `onLeave`). Votes can still change until `votingSeconds` runs out. Tests: `party-prompts-session`, `game-sessions`.
- 2026-09-01: **Wheel of Fortune (third game)** — turn-based puzzle on the same session shell as Word Scramble / Party Prompts: 4-letter codes, lobby floor of **two**, per-turn clock, puzzle wall-clock, late join sits out the current puzzle, and the board lock is `wheel.fortune` (house Priorities still decide jump/hold). Classic rules: consonants pay the wedge × count, vowels cost $250, BANKRUPT / LOSE A TURN / FREE SPIN, only the solver banks (\$1,000 floor). 382 board-fit puzzles. Command `wheel.invite`, route `/api/push/wheel-of-fortune`, settings at `/api/wheel-of-fortune/settings`. Yellow-chip board cards; hidden letters are white chips. `/games/` gained `wheel.js`. Cache-bust admin `signal282` / games `wheel.js`. Tests: `wheel-of-fortune`, `wheel-of-fortune-session`, `vestaboard-wheel-of-fortune`, `command-registry`, `vestaboard-holds`, `vestaboard-priorities`, `web-server`.

Nested `.push-card .push-card-icon` keeps the library and dashboard glyphs in the same box. Cache-bust `signal281` / `app.js` `signal274`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-09-01: **Party Prompts (second game) + game framework** — `games/sessions.js` is now game-agnostic: per-game `source`, a new **`voting`** phase, a `minPlayers` floor that closes a thin lobby on a *not enough players* card, and every game-shaped decision delegated to `games/modes/*`. Word Scramble moved into `modes/scramble.js` unchanged. Party Prompts ships 354 board-fit prompts, write-then-vote rounds (one vote is one point, never your own), an anonymous shuffled ballot, and violet board cards for invite / lobby / prompt / voting / round winner / final scores + best answer. Command `prompts.invite`, route `/api/push/party-prompts`, settings at `/api/party-prompts/settings`. - 2026-08-31: **Autodarts link no longer dies every few days** — keep-alive already refreshed ~every 10 minutes, but a failed refresh called `stopKeepAlive()` and a later device-link persist no-op'd the scheduler, so after one re-link the refresh token sat idle until Autodarts expired it. Persist now restarts keep-alive; a refresh that omits `refresh_token` keeps the current one; only 401 / invalid_grant mark re-link. Cache-bust `?v=signal237`. Tests: `autodarts-credentials`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Global Settings row lines up** — Dwell, **Save**, and **Priorities** share one 38px control height; Save sits next to the 3-digit box and Priorities stays on the right. Cache-bust `?v=signal236`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Now actually cuts in after the flap window** — a Ring (or any Now) jumper that arrived while a rotation page was still posting used to get Settings dwell put back on, so it sat at the front of the queue until the 60s page wait rather than flipping as soon as the Local API allows (~15s). The Next flip pill and queue row (**cutting in**) now follow the flap window, not leftover dwell. Cache-bust `?v=signal235`. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Settings Global Settings band** — Settings → Vestaboards drops the Local API intro. House dwell and Priorities sit under **Global Settings** with a 3-digit dwell box (max 999s) and a **Save** button. Cache-bust `?v=signal234`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **House-wide Vestaboard queue, dwell, and priorities** — one pending line for every enabled board. Every Vestaboard-capable event fans out even if one board is selected. Quiet hours stay per-board at post time. Cache-bust `?v=signal233`. - 2026-08-31: **Priorities “Now” cuts in immediately** — each listed event can **Now** (replace what is showing as soon as the flap rate window allows) separately from merely jumping the waiting line. House defaults keep Now on for alarms and live games; uncheck it to wait out the current page. Cache-bust `?v=signal232`. Tests: `vestaboard-priorities`, `vestaboard-queue`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Priorities event names match Push** — catalog labels come from each command’s Push `title` (Ring Doorbell, Steam, Huupe Live, …) instead of shortened specials like “Doorbell”. Live-only interrupts (Alarm fired, Spoken announce) keep their own names. Tests: `vestaboard-priorities`.
- 2026-08-31: **Simulator hold pill says Holding; Release Holds is house-wide** — a Feature Presentation (or any) hold no longer labels the orange chip “Waiting on game”. **Release Holds** on the Queue card calls `POST /api/vestaboards/release-holds` and clears lane locks on every board, not only the simulator. Cache-bust `?v=signal231`. Tests: `vestaboard-sim-api`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Priorities “Use recommended” confirm stacks on top** — the confirm sheet was earlier in the DOM than the Priorities sheet, so equal `z-index` painted it underneath. Cache-bust `?v=signal230`.
- 2026-08-31: **Recommended Vestaboard hold timeout is 30 minutes** — Word Scramble / Huupe / Autodarts (and the game-lock fallback) use a 30-minute safety net out of the box; sessions should still release earlier when detectors see the game end.
- 2026-08-31: **Priorities Add-an-event is the full board catalog** — Roast Me, Dad Jokes, and every other vestaboard-capable push are listed (grouped like Push). **Use recommended** asks for confirmation first. Cache-bust `?v=signal229`.
- 2026-08-31: **Simulator flip animation was racing itself** — `sim.state` right after `sim.flip` snapped every cell before its stagger delay set `is-flipping`, so you heard the cascade with no flaps. Same-target state updates now leave an in-flight (or pending) walk alone; settle still cleans leftover glyphs. Cache-bust `?v=signal228`.
- 2026-08-31: **Priorities sheet is denser** — one compact row per event (reorder · name · Hold · minutes · ×), wider sheet, Add/Save side by side. Cache-bust `?v=signal227`.
- 2026-08-31: **Per-board Vestaboard priorities** — Settings → Vestaboards → **Priorities** sets who jumps the line and who may hold it. House defaults: alarms / timers / reminders / doorbell / announce jump then the queue continues; Word Scramble, Huupe, and Autodarts hold with a 15-minute safety timeout. Unlisted events (YouTube / Plex / Steam / PSN included) just queue. Drag to order jumpers; Hold + max minutes is optional. Cache-bust `?v=signal226`. - 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Simulator can clear the whole queue** — **Clear queue** sits on the Queue card and only appears when pages are waiting. One tap drops every pending page; whatever is already on the board stays. `POST /api/vestaboard-sim/queue/clear`. Cache-bust `?v=signal225`. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `vestaboard-sim-api`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard hold lanes** — Huupe live was `priority: alert`, so every shot stacked a new queue page and wiped rotation. Ownership is now ranked: **alerts** (alarm / timer / reminder / doorbell / spoken announce) jump the line without discarding the rest; they still skip the scheduler rotation gap; **games** (Word Scramble, Huupe, Autodarts) and **now-playing** (YouTube, Feature Presentation, Steam, PSN) hold until they end or an equal/higher lane arrives; score updates replace the live card. Alerts preempt games; last-played and `.close` release a watch/game lock. Weather alerts, launch cards, dashboards, Alexa music, and Overhead just queue. Cache-bust `?v=signal224`. Tests: `vestaboard-holds`, `vestaboard-queue`, `vestaboard-router`, `vestaboard-gaming`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Guest Book messages append on the Vestaboard** — every `guest.book` push used to set `replaceSource`, so a second guest wiped the first from the queue. Live sends and invites now join the line; host bulk release/replay still clears earlier guest pages on the first item only. Tests: `vestaboard-router`, `guest-book`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Simulator "Next flip" follows live game timers** — a Word Scramble lobby/round used to say Next flip now after the 15s flap window because game cards skip Settings dwell. The queue now keeps a display-only phase hold from each card's `holdSeconds`, the pill counts that down as **Next card in Ns**, and a queued follow-up page uses its `notBefore` instead. Cache-bust `?v=signal223`. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `vestaboard-sim-api`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Simulator blanks leftover letters after a flip** — a cell whose committed target was already blank (the ocean around the US Weather Map) skipped its walk, so letters from the previous page stayed on the flap. The painted face must match the target; a settle pass after the cascade forces code 0 to an empty glyph. Cache-bust `?v=signal222`. Tests: `vestaboard-sim-api`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Simulator queue columns line up** — source, title, and status sit on a shared CSS grid so a short `Timers` row no longer pulls “waiting” left of `Weather alerts`. Narrow viewports stack the command id under the title. Cache-bust `?v=signal221`. Tests: `web-server`.
- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Simulator queue stays live and drag works** — a stuck `pointercancel` on the drag handle used to leave the list grayed out and ignore every later `sim.queue` event. Tests: `game-sessions`, `word-scramble`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word Scramble keyboard mode keeps the grid on screen** — focusing the word box still let Safari leave a phone-width 4×4 sitting under the keyboard. Tests: `word-riddles`.

- 2026-08-31: **Vestaboard Settings dwell now holds rotation pages** — Dwell was saved on the board but the queue only paced on the 15s Local API flap window, so the simulator (and a real board) flipped every 15s with Dwell set to 60. Snapshots now wait `snapshotUntil` (`max` of that window and the flap cooldown). Alerts and live game cards still flip as soon as the flaps can move. The simulator "Next flip" pill uses the longer of the two. Cache-bust `?v=signal214`. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `vestaboard-sim-api`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word of the Day push no longer freezes the bridge** — `pickWord` / `resolveWords` ran `fitsBoard` (a full 6×22 layout) across all 161,858 shipped entries on every Push. That blocked the event loop and could OOM the process, which dropped the simulator SSE and marked the bridge offline. The corpus is already board-fit at build time, so runtime only filters by part of speech and looks up by id. Tests: `word-of-the-day`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word Scramble phone keyboard no longer hides the letters** — focusing “Tap the letters or type” used to let Safari scroll the field to the top of the visual viewport, leaving a blank half-screen under the keyboard. While typing, the page now locks to `--gm-vh` (`position: fixed`) and sizes the grid to `--gm-board-max` so the tiles stay above the box. Tapping the field after building a word on the tiles places the caret after the last letter. Cache-bust games `games.css?v=5` / `scramble.js?v=7`. Tests: `web-server`.

Corpus is `src/misheard-lyrics-lyrics.json`, built by `tools/build-misheard-lyrics.js` from a curated seed, Wikipedia, MondegreenBench, and a local KissThisGuy cache. Settings → News is the Chuck Norris manage sheet plus an artist field. Cache-bust `?v=signal207`. Tests: `misheard-lyrics` (three channel golden frames), `command-registry`, `web-server`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word Scramble board follow-ups stay ahead of the hold** — phones already moved to round 2 while the Vestaboard stayed on round 1 because `replaceSource: 'word.scramble'` dropped the unshown intermission score card when the next grid was queued, and a follow-up that lost `frame.source` was treated as held with everything else. Later phases now line up in front of parked non-game pages (scores, then the next grid); `replaceCard` only evicts stale phases at or below that rank; lock ownership also honours `gameSource` / `replaceSource`. The hold lifts only when the session ends. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `game-sessions`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word Scramble accepts any dictionary word the letters spell** — CAT and TAR (and anything else the tiles can assemble) were refused as "Not on the board" because the solver still required an 8-way Boggle path. A word now counts when every letter is on the board and no tile is spent twice. The shipped corpus merges ENABLE1, 12dicts 2of12inf, and words_alpha so everyday English is not missing. Phone highlighting follows the same letter-pool rule. Cache-bust games `scramble.js?v=6`. Tests: `word-scramble`.

- 2026-08-31: **The Vestaboard game lock is session-scoped and absolute** — the previous lock was inferred from the last posted game frame's `holdSeconds`, which failed three ways. The lobby card's hold ran out at the instant round one began, so everything parked behind the game rushed the board while the round card sat at the back of the queue. Any non-game page that did land wiped the lock for the rest of the session. And `priority: alert` (Huupe live, Autodarts) skipped the hold outright and discarded the pages the game was holding. `games/sessions.js` now takes an explicit lock through `hub.setGameLock` on every phase card and releases it in `closeSession` — the single path covering finish, admin stop, invite expiry, idle, and last player out. While held, nothing else reaches the board: manual Push, Air now, scheduler ticks, and alerts all queue, with alerts keeping their place at the head for when the lock lifts. Game cards go to the front of the line they are holding. A 15-minute deadline, renewed by each phase card, bounds a session that dies without closing. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`, `game-sessions`.

- 2026-08-31: **Manual Push waits out a live Vestaboard game** — Admin Push / Air now used `breakHold`, which cleared the Word Scramble lock and jumped the line. During `holdKind: game`, `breakHold` is ignored: manual and scheduler snapshots queue until the session ends (finish, admin close, or last player leaves). Alerts still preempt. Future games register their `source` in `games/registry.js`. Tests: `vestaboard-queue`.

- 2026-08-31: **Date Book preview no longer overpaints the list** — A recent scroll tweak let the compose row shrink (`flex: 0 1 auto`); the board preview kept its height and painted over the search box and events. Compose is `flex: 0 0 auto` again and the two-column grid uses `align-items: start`. Cache-bust admin `?v=signal206`. Tests: `web-server`.

- 2026-08-31: **Genre / part-of-speech ticks line up in columns** — Top 10 Movies genres and the Learn (Japanese + Portuguese/Spanish/French/German/Italian) level and part-of-speech groups were flex rows, so every tick sat where its own label ended and nothing aligned down the card. They now use `.check-columns`, a grid whose track minimum is the widest label plus the box and its gap: in a 443px settings column that is four across for parts of speech and three for genres, with levels sharing the column stops with the group below. Cache-bust admin `?v=signal205`. Tests: `web-server`, `settings-filter`.

- 2026-08-31: **Expanded feed corpora** — Rebuilt `roast-me-roasts.json` (1,526), `dad-jokes-jokes.json` (2,449), and `family-quotes-quotes.json` (1,576) from curated public-source runs through the house build scripts. Tests: `roast-me`, `dad-jokes`, `family-quotes`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word Scramble touch + board ownership** — Letter tiles only grey out letters already in the current word (tap last to undo); unused letters stay clickable and validity is checked on submit. A game lock (`holdKind: game`) holds every non-game queued page until the session clears; only multi-page sequence tails are dropped when a game starts. Intermission shows the round winner + running total; final sequences high scores then best word. Last player leaving closes the session. Cache-bust games `?v=5`. Tests: `word-scramble`, `game-sessions`, `vestaboard-queue`.

- 2026-08-31: **Word Scramble lobby shows `2 PLAYERS` on the invite** — joining the second seat used to post a different six-row card (`GAME CODE` / pin / `WAITING TO START`), so during the rate-window cascade the board still read as the invite with only the leading `2` and trailing `S` of `2 PLAYERS` flipped. Lobby now reuses the invite layout and only writes the seat count on the blank row; `framesFor` prefers `card` over `phase`. Tests: `word-scramble`.

- 2026-08-31: **Date Book and day-of designer fit short screens** — on a 1512×949 remote desktop the manage sheet's `overflow: hidden` plus a 280px list min-height left almost no room to scroll events, and Design mode cut the board off with no way to reach it. Both sheets now scroll under ~900px tall (sticky title; designer keeps a sticky Save/Undo bar), compress tool chrome on short viewports, and only pin the event-list scroller when there is height to spare. Cache-bust `?v=signal204`.
- 2026-08-31: **Shopping list adds with no ASR still flip the board** — "alexa add pumpkin pie from winco to shopping list" wrote the item to Amazon and into `shopping-list-cache.json` after a manual push, but never aired a `shopping-list.snapshot`. History around the add was only empty wake/arbitration/discarded-intent rows (`unmatched-activities.jsonl` at 00:02:29), and `ws-todo-change` only scheduled a history poll — which found nothing to parse. `PUSH_LIST_ITEM_CHANGE` already carries `itemCreated`/`itemDeleted` + `listId`; the listener now debounces past the history poll, fetches the live shopping list, diffs it against the cache via `planShoppingListChangePush`, and pushes when an item actually moved (skipping todo-list ids and no-ops). Dedup fingerprints add/remove by the item that moved so a later ASR capture of the same edit does not double-flip. Tests: `shopping-list`, `voice-event-dedup`.
- 2026-08-31: **Autodarts stopped lying about re-link and keeps its refresh token alive** — Settings showed "Re-link needed / invalid or expired refresh token", Re-link toasted "Autodarts linked", and nothing changed. Three bugs stacked. (1) `status.linked` was true whenever a refresh token existed on disk, including a dead one, so the device-code UI stayed hidden behind the Linked chrome and the approval code never appeared. `linked` now means a usable session (`refreshToken && !needsRelink`); a rejected refresh is `hasCredentials` only. (2) `beginDeviceLink` returned `linked: true` from that same stale token, so the toast celebrated before the user approved anything — it now reports linked only after THIS poll persists fresh tokens. (3) Access tokens last ~15 minutes and Autodarts rotates the refresh token on every refresh; the live WebSocket can stay up for days without calling `getAccessToken`, so the refresh token aged out after a few idle days. Auth now runs a keep-alive (default 10 minutes, or sooner from `refresh_expires_in` / access expiry) started with the service, stores `refreshExpiresAt`, and — if `AUTODARTS_EMAIL`/`PASSWORD` are set — recovers from an invalid refresh via password login instead of forcing a manual re-link. Cache-bust `?v=signal203`. Tests: `autodarts-credentials` (stale linked claim, keep-alive rotation, env recovery).
- 2026-08-31: **YouTube stopped claiming to be playing things nobody was watching** — the wall aired `youtube.now-playing` cards, badged NOW PLAYING with an elapsed clock counting up, for a video that had finished hours earlier. `youtube-history.json` held the proof: after a real 2h35m watch ended at 16:08, the same video opened **twelve** more sessions between 17:01 and 01:18, every one with `watchedSeconds: 0`, `positionSeconds: 0` and `startedAt === endedAt` — the shape `onStarted` writes when a session opens and never records a watch. Two independent causes, both fixed. (1) **A standstill was being read as playback.** `CONFIRMABLE_STATES` includes `Buffering`/`Starting` so a real pick need not wait 60–90s for the next `Playing` tick, but an Apple TV left on a finished video answers the 45s keep-alive with exactly that: a confirmable state at position 0, forever. `confirmIfDue` now also requires a true `Playing` tick **or** a scrubber that has moved past `provisionalPosition` (seeded wherever the video was first seen, and re-seeded on a pre-roll so an ad cannot look like progress). (2) **The Settings button called a stopped video "playing".** `btn-youtube-test-push` posts no mode, so `pushManualPreview` runs in `auto`, and auto initialised `mode = 'playing'` and then resolved the target from `currentPlayback()` — which deliberately reports Paused, Stopped and unconfirmed videos so the button does not air stale history. Nothing on that path looked at `session.state`. Mode is now decided by `isLivePlayback()` (state is `Playing`, confirmed or provisional): anything else records what was watched and airs honestly as `last-played`, and an explicit `now-playing` request refuses rather than inventing a card. Also: sessions gained a closer that does not need the device's cooperation — every existing one waits for an explicit Stopped, a different video or a dropped task, so a TV that vanished mid-video stayed "playing" forever and kept the scheduler's content check true. `sweepIdleSessions()` retires anything silent for 10 minutes and runs off the existing keep-alive timer. `lastPlayed()` now skips rows with nothing watched and no position, since a seeded-but-never-filled row is a watch in progress or one that never happened; the 42 such rows the bug left behind were cleared from `data/youtube-history.json` (backup alongside). The Vestaboard formatter was never at fault — `youtubeFrames` renders no badge and no clock, so the wording was always the Python overlay's. Tests: `youtube-lounge` (standstill refused, advancing scrubber confirms, twelve keep-alive re-announcements open nothing, idle sweep), `youtube-now-playing` (stopped and paused videos push as last-played, strict now-playing errors).
- 2026-08-30: **Settings layout primitives** — `.field-grid` (+ `-2/-3/-4`), `.settings-group-title`, `.check-grid` and `.settings-footer` in `styles.css`. A settings column on a desktop is 400px wide and a box you type "45" into is not, so related fields now share a line and only stack when the card is genuinely narrow; grid cells align on their bottom edge, which only holds if nothing trails the input, hence hints go in a `.field-span-all` cell. Applied to five cards with no logic touched — every id, `.trivia-check` wrapper and `hidden`-toggled block is unchanged: **Word Scramble** (four timing numbers four-up, play settings two-up, short link moved into the right column, actions in a card footer), **Word Riddles** (now on the full-width list, three controls on one row), **Location** (city/ZIP and unit/currency two-up, Resolved alongside), **Ring Doorbell** (three columns — account, alert copy + when-to-flip checkboxes two-up, preview — via `.ring-columns` at 720px and 1080px), **Roll Credits** (credentials + storage / media / playback, rebalanced so no column trails a gap). Cache-bust `?v=signal202`. Also: the guest links on `login.html` set no colour and were falling through to the browser's blue-then-purple defaults, so they now pin one colour across `:link`/`:visited` and carry the affordance on hover and `:focus-visible` instead. Every other anchor in the app was already covered — the landing tiles set `color: inherit` and the admin links all sit inside `.hint` or a `.btn`.
- 2026-08-30: **US Weather Map** — Vestaboard-only push + schedule (`us.weather-map`) matching the marketplace channel. The board is the map: 89 colour chips in a fixed 22×6 silhouette of the lower 48 and not one letter. The mask was traced off the channel's own cards, which agree flap for flap — note the notch at row 0 columns 15-19 (the Great Lakes) with New England hanging off to its right, and the two lonely cells on row 5 (the tips of Texas and Florida). Those two tips are what the equirectangular fit is anchored on; the third corner then falls out at -122.3, which is Puget Sound, so the projection is the channel's own. **White is the cold end of the temperature scale, not the middle** — the channel's winter card is white across the north and blue across the south, which only reads that way if white is colder than blue. Six bands for six chips: white / blue / green / yellow / orange / red. A second `conditions` mode maps WMO codes, which is what the channel's noisy card is doing. Data is one keyless Open-Meteo call for all 89 points (comma-separated coordinates, array answer in the same order) cached for `refreshMinutes`; an outage falls back to the stale map, and a map with any hole in it is refused rather than aired half-drawn. Settings → News picks the mode, draws the legend, and **Push Now** forces a fresh read and mirrors it into the preview bezel. Cache-bust `?v=signal202`. Tests: `us-weather-map`, `command-registry`, `web-server`.
- 2026-08-30: **Dad Jokes** — Vestaboard-only push + schedule (`dad.jokes`) matching the marketplace channel. The card is two blocks with a row of air between them: the setup wrapped greedily at **21** columns, the blank row, then the punchline wrapped the same way, all left-aligned on column 0 and centred down the six rows. The blank row is not decoration — it is the pause before the groan — so `jokeLines()` emits it and the formatter drops it only when a long joke would otherwise open a second frame on an empty row. The rainbow card pins both layout constants at once: at 22 columns the punchline packs onto `IT'S PRETTY`, and with the house orphan rule the setup breaks after `HOW MUCH DOES`, so the wrap is 21 with `orphans: false`. Corpus is `src/dad-jokes-jokes.json`, built by `tools/build-dad-jokes.js` from public dad-joke collections; one-part jokes are dropped because they have nothing to put under the line. Settings → News is the Chuck Norris manage sheet with setup and punchline fields on the compose form and on every row. Cache-bust `?v=signal202`. - 2026-08-30: **Family Quotes** — Vestaboard-only push + schedule (`family.quotes`) matching the marketplace channel. - 2026-08-28: **Feature Presentation drops a zombie Plex session** — powering off the Apple TV often leaves `/status/sessions` in `playing` with a frozen `viewOffset`, so Settings stayed on **Playing**. Tests: `test/plex-now-playing.test.js`.
- 2026-08-28: **Vestaboard recreate no longer 503s the first post** — a container restart forgot the in-memory `lastPostAt` while the simulator still had flaps in the 15s window, so the first tick hit Local API rate-limit and waited. The queue now restores `lastPostAt` from `data/vestaboard-runtime.json` and the sim's `lastAcceptedAt`, and waits instead of probing. A 503 remains ordinary back-pressure if it still happens. A **?** next to Plex token explains how to copy `X-Plex-Token` from Plex Web XML ([Plex support](https://support.plex.tv/articles/204059436-finding-an-authentication-token-x-plex-token/)). Cache-bust `?v=signal113`. Tests: `web-server.test.js`.

- 2026-08-27: **Vestaboard call log POST column drifted with detail width** — the meta band used `auto` width, so a longer detail sentence widened column one and shoved **POST** sideways on the next row. Tests: `web-server.test.js`.
- 2026-08-27: **Vestaboard call log rows read cleaner** — timestamp and the detail sentence stack on the left; **POST** + endpoint and **200 flipped** sit in the middle and right columns, vertically centered against the two-line block. Cache-bust `?v=signal108`. Tests: `web-server.test.js`.
- 2026-08-27: **Vestaboard simulator call log columns drifted** — each row used `justify-content: space-between`, so a longer endpoint shoved **POST** left or right relative to the row above. API call rows now use a four-column grid (time · verb · endpoint · result) with the detail line spanning full width. Cache-bust `?v=signal107`.
- 2026-08-27: **Vestaboard weather spacing** — the NOW and HIGH/LOW rows sit one row lower with a blank band under **WEATHER** and another above tomorrow's footer, matching the physical board's breathing room.
